tamiḻar pōṟṟum mānilam, tamiḻar piṉpaṟṟum moḻi, tamiḻar mēṟkoṇṭa camayam, tamiḻariṭit tavaḻntuvanta māyap paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ yāvum tamiḻarkku ūṟu taruvatiyum; tamiḻariṭiyē tamiḻārāycci viṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭum cilar uṇarcciyāl tamiḻarallātārāyiruppatum kaṇṭu, tamiḻukku mutaliṭam eṉṟa kiḷarcciyiyum, tamiḻarkkē tamiḻakam eṉṟa kiḷarcciyiyum, aṟivum uṇarcciyum vīramum mikka tamiḻar tōṟṟuvittaṉar.
ivviyakkam mikap paḻantamiḻk koḷkiyiyum, paṇpāṭṭiyum mīṇṭum ēṟpaṭutta muyalvatu, paḻimip paṟṟāl aṉṟu. appaḻimiyil kalantuḷḷa perumi nōkkiyum, atil aminta  tamiḻar uyir nilit tuṭippiyum ūkkattiyum kaṇṭumēyākum. atu putup puraṭci vēkattuṭaṉ eḻuntu karuttu vēṟṟumikaḷ, camaya vēṟṟumikaḷ, kaṭci vēṟṟumikaḷ ākiya ellāk karikaḷiyum uṭittup peruveḷḷamākap poṅkivarukiṟatu. ataṉit taṭippaṭutta yārālum-ivvīrat tamiḻarālkūṭa iṉi ākātu.
tamiḻar moḻiyi avarkaḷ cemmippaṭutta eṇṇiṉar. etirppu iruntatu. tamiḻakañ cārnta ayal nāṭukaḷ ittakiya etirppukaḷāl kulintē tamiḻp paṇpiṉiṉṟu naḻuviṉa eṉpati ammoḻiyiṉ varalāṟukaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, tamiḻiṉ nalla kālam; moḻi ceppamuṟṟē varukiṟatu. tamiḻar kaṇ kuḷira, etirppavar kaṇṇīr uṇṇīrāka oṭuṅkat taḻittē varukiṟatu.
tamiḻarkkut tamiḻiyakkattār araciyal viḻippiyum eḻucciyiyum ūṭṭiṉar. etirppu mikuntatu. piḷavukaḷ malintaṉa. āṉāl, iṅkum tamiḻaṉṉi aruḷāl tamiḻ makkaḷ uḷḷūra oṟṟumiyaṭintu varukiṟārkaḷ.
camayat tuṟiyilē tamiḻiyakkam taliyeṭuttatu. ‘nammi vaḷartta moḻiyi nām ceppam ceytōm; nām vaḷartta araciyali aṭimi niliyi lum ceppam ceytu varukiṟōm. nāmē paṭittu vaḷartta nam camayattiyum iṉic ceppam ceyvōm eṉṟeḻuntaṉar vīrattamiḻ marapiṉar.
moḻik kōṭṭi maṇ kōṭṭi; atil pakivar patuṅka iṭamilli. araciyal kōṭṭi muḷ kōṭṭi. atil vīrattamiḻaṉ kāl kiḻiya naṭantāṉ. pakivar muḷ mēl kāl vikka añci vaḻiviṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, camayakkōṭṭi matilum, ñāyilum, curaṅka aṟikaḷum uṭiyatu. kōṭṭi yiṉuḷ  iruppavarkku māṟā veṟṟi tarumpaṭi amintatu. āyiṉ. antō iṉṟu kōṭṭikkuriyavar uḷḷirukka villi. naṇpar cilari nampip pātukākka viṭṭu viṭṭu veḷit taṅkiyuḷḷaṉar. kōṭṭi matilkaḷ pōli naṇpar eṟipaṭikaḷ vīcum iṭamāyiṉa. curaṅkaṅkaḷ  paṭikkalaṅkaḷ avarkaḷ kiyil! kōṭṭiyiṉ koṭikaḷkūṭa iṉṟu avarkaḷ koṭikaḷ! tamiḻiyakkat tār immuṟiyil pōrāṭa vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu.
tamiḻiyakkattār tamiḻar camaya vāḻvic ceppam ceyya muyaṉṟu cōrvuṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉar. tam kōṭṭi tam etiri vacamāṉāl ati aḻikkat tāṉē vēṇṭum eṉkiṉṟaṉar cila iṉamaṟavar. āṉāl, āṇṭu mutirnta cilar, antō nam piḷḷikaḷ, nam mātar kōṭṭiyiṉuḷ uḷḷaṉarē. avarik karutiyēṉum aḻivu tavirttal vēṇṭumē eṉṟu kavalkiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻar camayam mācēṟiyuḷḷatu. kaḻuviṉāl mācu pōkum. āṉāl, kaḻuvum nili kaṭantuviṭṭatu. mācu paṭippaṭiyāy ēṟi uṭalil nōyp puḻu malintu viṭṭatu. nōy tōl kaṭantu, taci kaṭantu, kurutiyil paravi, elumpil tōyntu viṭṭatu. pala uṟuppukaḷ puṟṟēṟiṉa. nērattil kaḻuvātavaṟṟi ūciyiṭṭu māṟṟi yirukkalām; kālattil ūciyiṭātavaṟṟi aṟuviceytu māṟṟi yirukkalām. ippōtu oṉṟiraṇṭu uṟuppēṉum aṟutteṟiyappaṭutal vēṇṭum niliyi lirukkiṟatu. uṟuppi aṟutteṟivatā? eṉṟu āra amarntu celvar tayaṅki ṉāl, pōvatu iṉi uṟuppaṉṟu, uyirpputtāṉ.
tamiḻar camayattiṉ mācā? etu mācu, etu uṭal?
tamiḻiyakkattār tamiḻar camayam etu eṉṟu kāṇum vakiyil oṟṟumip paṭavilli. oṟṟumip paṭavum muṭiyātu. tamiḻar camayam etu eṉṟu kaṇṭu ataṉi ākkum vēli vēṇṭuvatilli. tamiḻar camayattil āvaṉa koṇṭu allaṉa nīkki avaravar muyaṉṟāl pōtum!
āyiṉ āvaṉa koḷḷa yārum taṭiceyyavilli. ‘uṉakku murukaṉā vēṇṭum! yār taṭukkiṟārkaḷ? kāḷiyā vēṇṭum! yār taṭukkiṟārkaḷ? ivvākka vēlikku viḷamparam pōtum; pōr vēṇṭuvatilli. 
āyiṉ; allaṉa vilakkutal appaṭiyalla. oruvaṉukku āvatu, aṭutta vaṉukku āṉālum, ākāviṭṭālum keṭutalilli. āyiṉ, allaṉa vaṟṟi nīkkutal vēṇṭumāṉāl aṉivarum nīkkutal vēṇṭum.
vīṭṭukku oru māṭi, iru māṭi, ēḻu māṭi vippatāl ayalāṉukkuk kēṭilli. āṉāl, iṭintu viḻumpaṭi kaṭṭiṉāl?
etu camayam eṉpatil, tamiḻariṭiyē, tamiḻiyakkat tāriṭiyēkūṭa oṟṟumiyilli. civam tamiḻ eṉpār oruvar, aṉṟeṉpār maṟṟoruvar. āṉāl, itu tāvilli (kēṭilli)! etu tamiḻar camayam aṉṟu eṉpatil tamiḻaṟiñar ellārumē oṟṟumippaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. kīḻkkāṇum ceytikaḷil mutal nāṉku tamiḻar (kiṟi°var mukammatiyar uḷpaṭa) yāvarum oppuk koḷḷat takkavi. maṟṟa iraṇṭu uṇarcci mēlīṭuḷḷa cilar (nalleṇṇat tuṭaṉēyē) maṟukkak kūṭiyaviyākalām.
1. 	tamiḻariṭiyē piṟappiṉāl uyarvu tāḻvu kiṭiyātu.
uyarvu tāḻvukaḷ iyaṟkiyāy ēṟpaṭuvatuṇṭu. iyaṟkiyākavum muyaṟciyālum avi pōkum. āṉāl, iṉṟu tamiḻariṭiyē ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa uyarvu, ivarkaḷ iṉṟiya civaṉ kōvil, perumāḷ kōvil tāḻvu muṟṟi lum iyaṟkiyāṉatu eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉṟāl atu iyaṟkiyāy aḻivatiyum muyaṟciyāl aḻikkappaṭuvatiyum etirppavarkaḷ irukkiṟārkaḷ.
2. 	tamiḻariṭiyē iṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭirukkum uyarvu tāḻvu tamiḻaril oru cārārē ēṟpaṭuttiya uyarvu tāḻvalla. ēṉeṉil, tamiḻp paṇpu kuṟintu aṭimit taṉmi mikuntōṟum uyarvu tarappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻp paṇpu mikuntōṟum aṭimi uṇarcci kuṟiyum tōṟum tāḻvu ēṟpaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻ nāṭṭup paḻam pāṇar kuṭiyē vīrarip pāṭāmal kaṭavuḷip pāṭi avar peyarākiya tiriyil maṟiviliruntu vāḻum naccaravi aṇṭit tēvaraṭiyāḷāy tēvaṭiyāḷ kuṭiyāyiṟṟu. paktarukku maṟupatamāṉa tēvaraṭiyār, tācar eṉpavaṟṟiṉ peṇpālē tēvaṭiyāḷ tāci eṉṟākiyiruppatum; tamiḻ nāṭṭilum cari, intiya mānilattiṉ vēṟenta nāṭṭilum cari, pakta vakuppāraṉṟi maṟṟavarkaḷ ‘tācar’ eṉṟu peyar kūṭa vittatilli eṉpatum kāṇka.
aracarkku amiccarāyirunta vaḷḷuvar kuṭiyē-teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvar piṟanta tirukkuṭiyē-iṉṟiya paṟiyar kuṭi; avarkaḷ iṉṟu tīṇṭappaṭātavar.
iṉṉum itupōl piṟa āyntuṇaralām.
3. 	toḻilāl ēṟpaṭṭa nāl vakuppāṉa antaṇar, aracar. vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉpavaruḷ nālvarum piṟappāl orē  vakuppiṉarē. vēḷāḷar eṉṟa colliṉ vērākiya ‘vēḷ’ kaṭavuḷiyum, pōrppaṭiyiyum, uḻupaṭiyiyum, kuṟittalāl, vēḷvi vaḻipāṭṭiyum, vēḷāḷar antaṇariyum vēḷir aracariyum, vēḷāḷar vaṇikariyum, uḻavariyum uṇarttum collākiṉṟatu.
vēḷāḷarē camayattīkkipeṟṟu antaṇarāvar. pōr veṟṟiyāl araca marapiṉar āvar. toḻilāl vaṇikarum uḻavarum āvar. eṉavētāṉ vēḷāḷar uyarvākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉar. itu piṟappālaṉṟu. toḻilāl. (iṉṟu vēḷāḷa riṭiyē celva niliyiluḷḷār nāṉkām vakuppiṉarākavum uḻutu vāḻpavar aintām vakuppiṉarākavum ākiyiruttal kavaṉikkattakkatu.)
4. 	tuṟavōr, antaṇar eṉpavarkaḷ tamakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷar. (piṟappoṭṭiya evvakuppiṉarum allar; atilum koli vēḷvi ceytali ātarittuc cāti vēṟṟumiyi aṟamākavum camaya aṭippaṭik koḷkiyākavum koṇṭa vakuppiṉar allavē allar!)
tuṟavaṟam illaṟattukku māṟāṉataṉṟu. illaṟan tuṟanta aṟamum aṉṟu. taṉṉalam tuṟanta tuṟavaṟamēyākum. tam illattiṉ kaṭami muṟṟuṟṟapiṉ ulakukku uḻikka vantavartām  tuṟavōr. tam ilvāḻvil niṉṟē ulaku pēṇali mēṟkoṇṭavar antaṇar. piṟkālattil kōvil vaḻipāṭu mutaliya potukkaṭaṉāṟṟum ūḻiyaṉ pārppāṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. kurukkaḷ, ivarkaḷ piṟappāl tamiḻarākiya vēḷāḷarātalālum, koli vēḷvi ceyyātavarātalālum vēḷāp pārppaṉar eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉar. iṉṟum ivarkaḷuṭaṉ pirāmaṇar uṇṇuvatum maṇuṟavu koḷvatum illi. avarkaḷ mantiraṅkaḷum pirāmaṇar mantiraṅkaḷ alla. vēḷāḷar tīkki peṟumpōtu ōtum mantiraṅkaḷē. kurukkaḷ peyarākiya pārppāṉ eṉṟa collē tavaṟutalāyp piṟkālattil pirāmaṇaṉukku  vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. accol kuṟitta tamiḻar matippup pirāmaṇarkkup pōyiṟṟu, tamiḻar kuṭiyik kuṟitta accollip pirāmaṇar virumpātu ēḷaṉam ceytatāl atu kurukkaḷi iḻivupaṭuttum collāy atu māṟiṟṟu.
5. 	irappatu iḻivu. īki ciṟantatu.
kuṟippu:  ivviraṇṭum oṉṟuṭaṉ oṉṟu poruntā nītikaḷ. tirukkuṟaḷ mutaliya nūlkaḷil iraṇṭu nītikaḷum tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, evarum irattal kūṭātu, irattal iṟivaṉ paṭippilēyē iruttal takātu eṉṟu vaḷḷuvar perumāṉ kūṟuvataṉāl īki iṭamaṟintu utavutal eṉṟa poruḷē tarum. atāvatu poruḷīṭṭa muṭiyātavaṉ, muyaṉṟum peṟātavaṉ,  iṭukka ṇuṟṟavaṉ, piṟarkkeṉa uḻittut taṉṉip pēṇātavaṉ, potu nalam pēṇi āḷum aracaṉ ākiyavarkku ītaliyē tamiḻar īki eṉṟu koṇ ṭiruttal vēṇṭum. cōmpar, irattalit toḻilākak koṇṭavar. iṟivaṉ pēr collip piḻikkum ettar, irattal uyarveṉak koḷpavar, makkaḷiṭiyē vēṟṟumi viḷivippavar ākiyavarkaṭkuk koṭutta, koṭuttu varum tavaṟṟiṉālēyē tamiḻar nalintaṉar, nalikiṉṟaṉar.
6. 	kaṭavuḷ oruvarē, avar uruvamaṟṟavar, camayaṅ kaṭantavar (eṉavē ec camayattukkum avar uriyavar allar) avar kuṇam,  kuṟi,  ūr, pēr aṟṟavar (eṉavē āṇ peṇ pālvēṟupāṭu, cāti vēṟupāṭu ākiyavi avar peyarāl ēṟpaṭamuṭiyātu. avarkkut taṉiyāka oru tāynāṭum tāymoḻiyum kiṭiyātu.)
kuṟippu 1 :- ik kaṭavuṭ koḷki uyarntatēyāyiṉum, ik kaṭavuḷiṉ aruḷ niliyit tāmum koṇṭutāṉ pōlum tamiḻar tam paṇpiḻantu piṟar paṇpu pēṇi yaḻivuṟṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇātirukka muṭiyavilli. ‘aruḷum aruḷuṭiyār kaṇṇatē’ eṉpatit tamiḻar ōrātiruntaṉar, irukkiṉṟaṉar.
kuṟippu 2:- tamiḻarkkuc camayam illi eṉṟu kūṟuvōr coṟkaḷ paḻantamiḻar kaṭavuṭ koḷkikku avvaḷavu muraṇāyilli eṉpatu kāṇka. araciyalil ‘āḷāta araciyalē nalla araciyal’ eṉpar. ‘camayattilum camayamākāta camayamē uyar camayam’ eṉṉalākum.
caṅka ilakkiyattil āḻnta camayap paṟṟu uṇṭu. camayap paṟṟilāḻtal illi. tuṟaviyākiya iḷaṅkō ellāc camayamum pāṭi naṭunili niṉṟami ikkālattuṟaviyarāl muṭiyumā? tuṟaviyallātavarāltāṉ muṭiyumā?
7. 	kaṭavuḷukkup piṟappum iṟappum vāḻvum illi. avar aruḷvaliyāl (taṟkāla moḻiyil iyaṟki yāṟṟalāl) uyirkaḷum ulakaṅkaḷum naṭipeṟuvataṉṟi avarkaḷi allatu avaṟṟit tōṟṟuvippatu milli, aḻippatum illi (camaya moḻiyil kūṟiṉāl avar cāṭci māttiramāy niṉṟu iyakkukiṟār.) uyirkaḷukku avar ceyvatellām tuṉpattiṉiṉṟu viṭutalipeṟa utavuvatutāṉ.
ikkaruttukaḷukku māṟupaṭṭa camayam tamiḻar camaya mākātu. māṟupaṭṭa karuttukaḷum tamiḻar camayak karuttukaḷākā.
iṉṟiya tamiḻar camayattil mēṟkaṇṭa karuttukaḷ illi eṉṟu yārum collamāṭṭārkaḷ. ēṉeṉil, accamaya nūlkaḷiliruntē ivi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.
āṉāl, ivaṟṟukku māṟāṉa karuttukaḷum iṉṟiya tamiḻar camayattil eṇṇaṟṟavi. avaṟṟuḷ cila, cāti vēṟupāṭu, tīṇṭāmi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ātaravu, vaṭamoḻi teyvamoḻi eṉal, vaṭamoḻi mantiram ōtutal, cōmparkkum, ettarkkum ītal, tamiḻar camayattukku etirāṉa koḷki yuṭiya pirāmaṇarkaṭkuk kōvilil nuḻivurimi maṭṭumaṉṟit talimiyum koṭuttal ākiyavi. tamiḻ nūlkaḷil pārppaṉar kuṭiyiruppuc cēri eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ūrukkup puṟampē pul vīṭukaḷil vāḻntaṉar avarkaḷ. kōvili avarkaḷukku viṭṭup paktar (tēvaraṭiyār)kaḷukkup pātukāppāka avarkaḷi mutal teruvilum viṭṭa ceyalukkut tamiḻar etuvum paṭal vēṇṭum!
ic ceyal ceytavar tamiḻ aracarē-ciṟappākap piṟkālac cōḻar, nāyaṉmār kālattilum cēkkiḻār kālattilum irunta cōḻarē eṉpati nōkka avarkaḷ vīrattip pukaḻvatā, vīṇ vampukku ikaḻvatā eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyāta niliyilirukkiṟōm.
intu matam eṉṟu iṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatu civa neṟiyiyum tirumāl neṟiyiyum cērttutāṉ. āṉāl, ivi kalappaṟṟa civa neṟiyum tirumāl  neṟiyum alla. avaṟṟuṭaṉ kalantu niṟkum mūṉṟām neṟi yoṉṟu uṇṭu.  anneṟi cila vakiyil civa, tirumāl neṟiyi viṭac ciṟantatu. anneṟiyil orē oru cātitāṉ uṇṭu. (āyiṉum anneṟiyil cērāta civa neṟiyāḷar tirumāl neṟiyāḷarkaḷiṭiyē cāti vēṟupāṭṭi ēṟpaṭutti vaḷarppatu avarkaḷ uyirnilik kaṭami) anneṟik keṉṟu kōvilum uruvamum kiṭiyātu. (āṉālum taṉakkeṉak kūṭillāmal tāṉ muyaṉṟu ūṭṭāmal, kākkiyiṉ kūṭṭil kākki ūṭṭa vaḷarvatākak kūṟappaṭum kuyil kuñcukaḷ pōla, civar viṇavar celavil eḻuppappaṭṭa  kōvilkaḷil avarkaḷ uruviṉiyē vittuk koṇṭu avarkaḷ celavil vāḻntu avarkaḷ teyvattiṉ peyari vittē avarkaḷi eṉṟeṉṟikkum aṭippaṭutta vaḻi kaṇṭavarkaḷ anneṟi yāḷarkaḷ). poruḷum uyirum uḷḷa civa, tirumālneṟi vētaṅkaḷi ōtupavarkaḷi iḻivu paṭuttit tam poruḷaṟṟa, pitaṟṟal vētatti vittu anneṟic celvar paṇamum paṟittu avarkaḷi avamatikkavum vaḻiceyvar.
im mūṉṟām neṟikkup peyar vaḻaṅkuvatilli. āṉāl, peyar uṇṭu. atuvē cumārttam eṉpatu.
cāti vēṟupāṭṭip pēṇutal, vaṭamoḻikku ellā vakiyilum uyarvu kāttal ivi ivarkaḷ toḻil.
cumārttar tirunīṟiṭṭum iṭāmalum civaṉ kōvili āḷāmal āḷvar. avar tammic civap pirāmaṇar eṉṟukūṭak kūṟikkoḷvatilli. civarkaḷ attakiya tirumaṇ pāvikaḷ eṉṟu avarkaḷ eṇṇiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
āṉāl, viṇavar kōvilil cellum cumārttar tammic cumārttar eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷvatilli. viṇavar eṉpar. nāmam kaṭṭāyam iṭṭuk koḷvar. āṉāl avar ‘kali’yāl avariyaṟiyalām. vaṭamoḻi pēṇum avarkku ‘vaṭakali’ eṉṟa peyar poruttam tāṉē!
viṇavattil kōvil kurukkaḷākiya teṉkaliyāṉ vivaram terintō teriyāmalō iṉṉum vaṭakaliyi etirppāṉ. civam paṇivallavā? civak kurukkaḷ civamākiya pirāmaṇaril ‘aikkiyamāy’ oṉṟāki vēṟāki niṟkiṟārpōlum!
civarum viṇavarum cumārtta neṟiyiyum vaṭa kaliyiyum, oḻittālaṉṟi avarkaḷ neṟi paḻiya tamiḻneṟi yākātu.
ivaṟṟi oḻittāl kōvil tamiḻ vētaṅkaḷē pāṭappaṭum. tamiḻ mantiraṅkaḷ ōtappaṭa vaḻi ēṟpaṭum. vaṭamoḻi vētam oḻikkappaṭa vaḻiyuṇṭu. cāti, tīṇṭāmiyi oḻikka muṟpaṭalām.
cumārttarkaḷiṭam kōyili viṭṭuviṭṭu, nalla uṇarcci mikka tamiḻar nā°tikarāṉāl, ‘kaṭavuḷē, inta nā°tikar pakkam niṉṟu ā°tikap pūṇṭi aḻi eṉṟutāṉ’ tamiḻar vēṇṭikkoḷḷal takuti.
7. tamiḻc coṟkaḷum tamiḻp paṇpāṭum
coṟkaḷ ulakap poruḷkaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ maṭṭumalla; karuttukaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷum kūṭa. poruḷkaḷik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ accoṟ poruḷi aṟinta makkaḷ uḷḷa nāṭṭu moḻikaḷilellām irukkum. āṉāl, karuttukaḷik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ ellā moḻikaḷilum orē niliyil iruppatilli. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, koṭuttal, tarutal, ītal; nām, nāṅkaḷ; vaṇakkam, vaḻipāṭu, toḻuki ākiya nuṭpa vēṟupāṭukaḷ āṅkilattil illi. kaṉavu, naṉavu, eṉṟa māṟupāṭum kāy, kaṉi ākiya vēṟupāṭum āṅki lattilum maṟṟem moḻiyilum illi. iṉṟiya pala āṅkilak karuttuk kuṟikaḷukkum itupōlat  tamiḻil pala camayam nēr taṉiccol illāma liruppatuṇṭu. iṅṅaṉam karuttuk kuṟikkum col vaḷattiṉ vēṟu pāṭṭā lēyē moḻikaḷiṭiyē paṇpāṭṭu vēṟupāṭum, uyarvu tāḻvum matikkappaṭum. tamiḻ ivvakiyil taṉit taṉmiyum uyarvum uṭiya teṉpatu ārāycci vakiyāl kāṇavēṇṭuvatoṉṟu.
moḻiyil eṉṉa irukkiṟatu eṉṟum collil eṉṉa irukkiṟatu, enta moḻiccolli enta moḻiyil vaḻaṅkiṉāl eṉṉa eṉṟum ippōtu pala arikuṟi matiyiṉar ārāyātu kūṟuvatuṇṭu. coṟkaḷ karuttukaḷik kuṟippavi; tamiḻil ellāc collum karuttuk kuṟippavi; karuttu aṟiviyal muṟippaṭi kūṟiṉāl, nīṭitta vāḻvuṭiya ōr uyiriyakkamuṭiya poruḷ eṉpatupōṉṟa uṇmikaḷik kavaṉippavar ivvāṟu koḷḷa muṭiyātu. moḻiyiṉ collil ammoḻiyāḷariṉ  iṟantakāla vāḻkkik karuttu muṟṟilum karuvūlamākac cēmikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ pala kāla māṟupāṭṭāl poruḷ tirintiruppatuṇṭu. aṟiñar tēvanēyappāvāṇar pōṉṟa ārāycciyāḷar palar attakiya māṟupāṭukaḷil palavaṟṟi eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. vaḷital eṉṟa poruḷil maṭṭumē caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ‘vāṅku’ eṉṟa vaḻaṅkum iccol ki vaḷintu peṟutal eṉṟa kuṟippil ‘peṟu’ eṉṉum  poruḷil iṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. itu ittakiya māṟutalukku ōr  eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
mēṟ kuṟippiṭṭatu pōṉṟa coṟporuḷ māṟupāṭu kālavēṟupāṭṭi maṭṭumē kuṟikkum. āṉāl, paṇpāṭṭu māṟupāṭṭik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷum pala uṇṭu. immāṟupāṭukaḷiṟ pala uṇmiyil tamiḻp paṟṟuṭiyavar uḷḷatti aṟukkum māṟupāṭukaḷ ākum. ēṉeṉil, ac coṟkaḷiṉ paḻam poruḷum putupporuḷum tamiḻaṉ muṉṉiya uyarviyum iṭikkāla vīḻcciyiyum aḷantu kāṭṭupaviyāyuḷḷaṉa.
ittakiya coṟkaḷil nūl, pāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉam, poṉ eṉpaṉa cila.
nūl
nūl eṉṟa col ippōtu  perumpālum cuvaṭi allatu puttakam eṉṟa poruḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. tirukkuṟaḷ oru nalla nūl, itu caṅka nūl eṉṟellām vaḻaṅkukiṟōm. āyiṉum vāṉanūl, kaṇakkunūl eṉṟu collumpōtu nām koḷḷum poruḷ vēṟu. iṅkē  atu aṟivunūl eṉṟō, aṟivunūl tuṟi eṉṟō poruḷpaṭum. ippintiya poruḷilēyē muṟkālattil iccol payaṉpaṭṭatu. nūl, nūlōr, kaṇakkāyar mutaliya coṟkaḷ muṟkāla ilakkiya vaḻakkil ilakkiyattiyō ilakkiya ēṭukaḷiyō kuṟittatākak kāṭṭavē muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil atu ārāycci muṭivukaḷi maṭṭumē kuṟittatu. ivvakiyil ‘nūl’ eṉṟa paḻantamiḻc colliṉ  poruḷ aṟiviyal (ḷuuninuna) eṉṟa āṅkilap poruḷē ākum. tamiḻariṭiyē ārāycci paḻaṅkatiyākap pōṉapiṉṉarē itu cuvaṭi eṉum poruḷil payaṉpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam poruḷ iḻivupaṭṭata ṉālēyē iṉṟu ikkarutti (ḷuuinēuna)k kuṟikkat tamiḻc collukkut  tiṇṭāṭukiṟōm. vaṭa moḻiyilkūṭa itaṟkuc cariyāṉa col kiṭiyavē kiṭiyātu. cā°tiram eṉpatu caṭṭam allatu vitikaḷiṉ tokuppu eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum.
nūl, nūṟpā eṉṟa coṟkaḷiraṇṭum toṭarpupaṭṭavi. nūliṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟum naṉṉūl iḻitta nūliṉ paṇpiyum, marakkōṭṭam aṟukkum nūliṉ paṇpiyum uvami kūṟiyuḷḷatu. itiliruntē nūl eṉpatu, kaṇakku vāṉiliāyvu,  iyaṟporuḷāyvu, moḻiyiṉilakkaṇam mutaliya aṟivut tuṟikaḷi maṭṭumē kuṟikkum eṉpatu teriyavarum.
itaṟkēṟpa nūṟpā eṉṟa collum nūlukkāṉa ‘pā’ eṉap poruḷpaṭukiṟatu. cilappatikārap pākkaḷum pattuppāṭṭup pākkaḷum pākkaḷ maṭṭumē; nūṟpākkaḷ alla. itiliruntē cilappatikāramum pattuppāṭṭum nūlkaḷ alla, ilakkiya ēṭukaḷ maṭṭumē eṉṟu kāṇalām.
nūl (ḷuuninuna) kiṭṭattaṭṭa iṟantuviṭṭa kālattil tōṉṟiya vaṭamoḻi nākarikam nūṟpāvic cūttiram eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. iccol (sūtram) uṇmiyil nūl eṉpataṉ moḻipeyarppu. nūliṉ paṇpu iḻakkappaṭṭa kālattavar ataṉ pā vaṭivattukku maṭṭum itaṉip payaṉ paṭuttiṉar. vaṭamoḻiyil cūttiram eṉpatu oru pāvaki, oru naṭivaki, āṉāl, tamiḻil atu oru taṉi vakippaṭṭa tuṟikkāṉa ceyyuḷ vaki, vaṭamoḻi muṟippaṭi tirukkuṟaḷiṉ kuṟaṭpākkaḷic cūttiram eṉṉalām. āṉāl, tamiḻil atu cūttiramum alla, nūṟpāvum alla. kuṟaṭpāvē.
makkaḷukku ilakkiyam, ārāycciyāḷarkku nūl. nūlārāyccikkum appāl ceṉṟu aṉupava meymmi kaṇṭavarkaḷ aṟivumaṟi, maṟi eṉpatu nūṉmuṭipu, meyyuṇarvu (ñāṭiṭiḷaṭiyīhla, ālaḷaviuḷia) ākiyavaṟṟik kuṟikkum.
caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkum muṟpaṭṭa, oru vēḷi, tolkāppiyat tukkum muṟpaṭṭa kālattiliruntē tamiḻarkaḷiṭiyē ārāyccittuṟi kuṉṟi ‘nūl’ eṉṟa collil māṟāṭṭamum kuḻappamum ēṟpaṭat toṭaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. āyiṉum, ilakkaṇa nūlōr ac collip perumpālum piṟa ḻāmal vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. uriyāciriyarkaḷum ivvēṟupāṭu kuṟittuḷḷaṉar.
ip poruḷ vēṟupāṭu muṟṟilum maṟakkappaṭṭa kālam etu eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātāyiṉum vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkaḷāl tamiḻp paṇpāṭu aṟiyappaṭu muṉṉarē ic coṟkuḻappa mēṟpaṭṭa teṉṉalām. tiruvaḷḷuvamāliyil nūlellām vaḷḷuvarcey nūlāmō eṉṟu kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.
pāṭṭu
ippōtu pāṭṭu, pā, ceyyuḷ ellām oru poruḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ etirccol āka vaḻaṅkuvatu urinaṭi.
uriyeṉpatu pāṭṭukku uri eṉṟa poruḷil piṟkālattu vaḻaṅkiyatum uṇṭu. paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ urinaṭiyi uric ceyyuḷ eṉṟaṉa. itiliruntē ceyyuḷ eṉpatu urikku etiriṭiyalla eṉṟu kāṇalām. uri eṉpatu pēccu. an naṭiyil ilakkaṇa ilakkiya varampamiyac ceyyappaṭṭatu uric ceyyuḷ. yāppamitiyum  amintāl atu yāppu allatu pāvaki ākum. ākavē ceyyuḷ eṉpatu iṉṟu nām ‘ilakkiyam’ eṉṟu kūṟum poruḷil vaḻaṅkiyatāyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟalām. pāvakiyil pāvēṟu, pāviṉam vēṟu eṉṟum vakukkappaṭṭaṉa.
aṟivu cāṉṟa karuttukaḷ nūlāka nūṟpāvil eḻutappaṭṭatu pōla; aṟivum uṇarcciyum aḻakum cāṉṟa karuttukaḷ pākkaḷāka eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. cilakāl pāviṉaṅkaḷākavum eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ ōciyiṉ  icinayam mikkatāṉa muṟiyē pāṭal, allatu pāṭṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. ākavē pāvēṟu, pāṭṭu vēṟu ākum. cilappatikāram pākkaḷālāṉatu. tēvāram pāṭṭukaḷālāṉatu. ip pāṭṭukaḷ pāvākavum pāviṉamākavum irukkumāyiṉum, pāviṉaṅkaḷ perumpālum icippāṭṭukaḷilēyē vaḻaṅkiṉa. tēvāram icippāṭal tokuti eṉpati maṟantu iciyuṇarvu keṭṭa piṟkālat tamiḻakam atik kāviyattukkum payaṉpaṭuttiṟṟu. ilakkiyam eṉṟa poruḷuṭiya ceyyuḷ eṉum col poruḷ māṟupaṭṭataṉālēyē ceyyuḷum nūlum cērntatu; iyal eṉṉum eṇṇam maṟantupōyiṟṟu. paḻam muttamiḻp pakuppiṉ parappi āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷuṭaṉ 47ām pakkam uḷḷa viḷakkap paṭivattil kāṇka.
viruttaṅkaḷ vaṭa moḻiyiliruntu vantavi eṉpatu palar karuttu. itu tavaṟāṉa karuttu. tamiḻil atu pāviṉamāy iciyil maṭṭum vaḻaṅkiyatu. kalippā uṟuppukaḷil varum vakikaḷ viruttaṅkaḷē. palavaki aṭikaḷilum eḻutappaṭṭa avi nāḷaṭivil nāṉkaṭi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉa. tamiḻarē itaṉi aḷavaṭi (ḷuvayaṉēyacaṉa rēaneca ṭika kananava) eṉṟaṉar. aṭi, kāl ākiyavaṟṟiṉ moḻipeyarppākavē vaṭamoḻi aṭi (pātam) amintiruppatum kāṇalām. vaṭamoḻiyilēyē viruttamuṟi piṟpaṭṭatu. avaṟṟil etuki mōṉi amintatu tēvārak kālattukkum piṟpaṭṭutāṉ. vēta itikācak kāla vaṭamoḻi yil mūṉṟaṭi, iraṇṭaṭiyē mikuti.
paṭṭiṉam
tamiḻar vaḻakkil paṭṭiṉam eṉpatu kaṭaṟkariyiluḷḷa tuṟimuka nakaram eṉap poruḷpaṭum. tamiḻar  nilatti aintiṇiyākki ovvoru tiṇiyilum ciṟṟūrukkum pērūrukkum taṉittaṉip peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉar. ivvēṟupāṭu ulakil vēṟu em moḻiyilum kiṭiyātu. ivvēṟupāṭu aḻivuṟṟa piṉṉarē vaṭamoḻi ilakkiya vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. ēṉeṉil, itaṉ cuvaṭṭi vaṭamoḻiyil kāṇamuṭiyavilli. vaṭamoḻip paṇpāṭu pintiya teṉpataṟku ivvetir maṟiccāṉṟu maṭṭumaṉṟu; nērmukac cāṉṟum uṇṭu.  paṭṭiṉam eṉṟa col kaṭaṟkarip pērūr eṉṟa poruḷ keṭṭatuṭaṉ eḻuttu uruvamum kuṟivuṟṟup paṭṭaṇam eṉa iṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. itu piṟpaṭṭa vaḻakku eṉṟu kūṟat tēviyilli. vaṭamoḻiyil ippiṟpaṭṭa vaḻakkum piṟpaṭṭa vaṭivumē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. attōṭu atu piṟamoḻi (tamiḻ)c collātalāl paṭṭaṇam, paṭṭaṉam, pattaṇam, pattaṉam eṉap palavāṟākavum eḻutap paṭṭatu. ivvaḻakkum mika arukalāṉatum piṟkālattatum ākum.
tamiḻar kuṟitta poruḷil paṭṭiṉam eṉṟa col iṉṟiya tamiḻ nāṭ ṭelliyil maṭṭumaṉṟip paṇṭit tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭiṉ muḻu elliyiyum kāṭṭi intiyā eṅkum akkuṟippu māṟāmalē iṭap peyarkaḷil vaḻaṅkap paṭukiṟatu. neytal nilac ciṟṟūrākiya pākkam, marutanilattup peyarākiya ūr ākiyavaṟṟippōlavē paṭṭiṉam eṉṉum peyarum intiyāveṅkum tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiṉ peyar īṟṟilēyē iruppatu kāṇalām. kāyal paṭṭiṉam, kulacēkaraṉpaṭṭiṉam, nākappaṭṭiṉam, cērappaṭṭiṉam, caturaṅkap paṭṭiṉam, ceṉṉippaṭṭiṉam, macūlippaṭṭiṉam, vicākappaṭṭiṉam ākiyavi kāṇka. kaṅkik kariyiluḷḷa pāṭṉā āṟṟut tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉam oṉṟik kuṟitta peyarēyākum.
poṉ
tamiḻil iṉṟu ulōkaṅkaḷik kuṟikkum potuc col illi eṉṟu tōṟṟum. āṉāl aimpoṉ eṉṟa vaḻakkum veṇpoṉ (veḷḷi) eṉṟa vaḻakkum uṇmiyil ipporuḷuṭiya col ‘poṉ’ eṉpatē eṉṟu kāṭṭum. ippotuc col taṅkattiṉ peyarāyk kuṟikkappaṭṭa kālam  etuvō aṟikilōm. āṉāl, ip peyariliruntē perumpālāṉa ulōkap peyarkaḷ vantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kāṇalām. vaṅkam, īyam, taṅkam, pittaḷi eṉṟa  nāṉku ulōkaṅkaḷukkum tamiḻar taṉip peyariṭṭaṉar. iviyē avarkaḷukku mutalil aṟiyavanta ulōkaṅkaḷ eṉṟukūṭa eṇṇalām. ivaṟṟip potuvākak kuṟikka avarkaḷ poṉ (polivuṭiyatu) eṉṟa colli vaḻaṅkiṉar. maṟṟa ulōkaṅkaḷi avarkaḷ niṟampaṟṟiyum paṇpu paṟṟiyum kāraṇap peyarākak kuṟittaṉar. cempoṉ (ūṭiyīyīnaca) civappu niṟamuṭiya poṉ, cempoṉ eṉpatē cempu eṉak kuṟukiṟṟu. veṇpoṉ(ḷuṭienaca) itupōl veḷḷi yāyiṟṟu. irumpoṉ (irumi=kaṟuppu) karumi niṟamuṭiya poṉ. itu irumpu eṉak kuṟukiṟṟu. taṅkam pimpoṉ allatu  pacumpoṉ (pacumi allatu meṉmi uṭiyatu) eṉappaṭṭatu. taṅkam eṉṟa col iruppatālum, poṉ eṉpatē poruḷ kuṟuki ataṉ peyarāy vaḻaṅkiyatālum itil colkuṟukkam ēṟpaṭavilli.
tamiḻar taṅkattil palavakit taṅkattikkūṭa aṟintiruntaṉar.


8. vaṭacoṟkaḷeṉa mayaṅka iṭantarum tamiḻccoṟkaḷ
moḻippaṟṟu, nāṭṭuppaṟṟu, camayappaṟṟu mutaliyavi makkaḷ vāḻkki yiṉ vaḷarccikkuc ciṟanta tūṇṭutalkaḷāy iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, palar avaṟṟi nērmiyaṟṟa kuṟukiya muṟiyil mēṟkoṇṭu avaṟṟiyē nākarika vaḷarccikkum ciṟappāka naṭunili ārāyccikkum muṭṭukkaṭṭi kaḷāka vaḻaṅki varukiṟārkaḷ. atilum ivvakiyil nam nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭum nilimi mikamikap putumiyāṉatu. potuvāka ulakil ārāyccikkut taṭi yākak karutappaṭum moḻippaṟṟu makkaḷ uṇarccikkut tāyakamāṉa tāy moḻip paṟṟēyākum. āṉāl, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ ārāycciyil kāṇappaṭum piṟpōkkut tāymoḻip paṟṟāl aṉṟu, piṟamoḻip paṟṟālēyē ākum. itu viyappuk kiṭamaṉṟō?
tamiḻmoḻiyum vaṭamoḻiyum āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷāka ivvintiyak kaṇṭattil oruṅkē vaḷarcci yaṭintu varukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉ paṇpukaḷ, coṟkaḷ, karuttukaḷ mutaliyavi iṉṉoṉ ṟilum iṭampeṟak kūṭum eṉṟu etirpārttal iyaṟkiyē. āṉāl, tamiḻil vaṭacoṟkaḷiyum  vaṭavar paṇpukaḷiyum karuttu kaḷiyum tēṭit  turuvit tokuppatil caḷikkāta aṟiñar palar vaṭa moḻiyil uḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷ, karuttukaḷ, paṇpukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟit tēṭa eṇṇuvatukūṭa illi. mēlināṭṭaṟiñar allatu vaṭa nāṭṭaṟiñar avaṟṟit tēṭi aṟivittālkūṭa aṉṉattiṉ iṟakukaḷil allatu tāmari yiliyil paṭṭa nīrāl avi īramaṭiyātatupōla, avarkaḷ karuttiyum ceyaliyum atu caṟṟum tākkāmal puṟakkaṇittut taḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.
vaṭamoḻip paṇṭitarkaḷum avarkaḷip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻp paṇṭitar palarum iṭikkālattil tamiḻilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam ākiya ellām vaṭamoḻi varavukaḷtām eṉṟu nilināṭṭa muyaṉṟaṉar. “aṉṟiyum ainteḻuttāl oru pāṭiyeṉṟaṟiyavē nāṇuvar aṟivuṭiyōrē” eṉṟu  tamiḻi iḻivu paṭuttit tammippōṉṟa tamiḻaṟiñariyum aṟivilātavarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiya ilakkaṇak kottu āciriyar ivvakuppiyē cērntavar āvar. mēl nāṭṭā ratu pakaloḷi katir erikkum innāḷkaḷil ip ‘paḻampāṭi’k karuttukaḷ paṭṭap pakal nilavupōl poliviḻantu pōyiṉa. āyiṉum cūrappaṉmaṉ ‘tiruvuru’kaḷ pōl avi palvēṟu uruvil iṉṉum vantu nammi maruṭṭuvatuṇṭu.
taṟpōtu tamiḻiṉamum āriya iṉamum vēṟu vēṟāṉa taṉippēriṉaṅkaḷ eṉṟa koḷki eṅkum oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. eṉavē, tamiḻiṉiṉṟu āriyam vantateṉṟō āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻ vantateṉṟō evarum kūṟa iṭamilli. eṉavē ivviru moḻikaḷiṉ uṟavellām ottuvāḻum kālattil ēṟpaṭṭa naṭpuṟavukaḷāyirukkak kūṭumē yallāmal, oṉṟukkoṉṟu tāyakamāṉa uṟavu etuvum irukka muṭiyātu.
iṉṟiya tamiḻilakkiyattiṉ tōṟṟakkālamutalē atāvatu namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa paḻantamiḻilakkiyak kālamutalē oru cila vaṭacoṟkaḷēṉum tamiḻilakkiyattil kāṇappaṭuva tōṭu avi varavarap perukiyum vantuḷḷaṉa. tirunālā yirattukku uri eḻutappaṭṭa kālattilum civa cittānta nūl kaḷukku viḷakka urikaḷ eḻunta civamaṭaṅkaḷ kālattilum vaṇṭik kaṇakkil vaṭacoṟkaḷi motta iṟakkumati ceyyum muyaṟcikaḷ ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. āṉāl, yār peṟṟa pēṟō ammuyaṟcikaḷ payaṉaṟṟup pōyiṉa. tamiḻilē maṭṭumaṉṟi, tamiḻiṉattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭa ayal moḻikaḷilum ovvoru kālattil ittakiya “maṇip piravāḷa” naṭi tōṟṟuvikka muyaṟcikaḷ naṭantu iṅkēpōl aṅkum avi tōlviyuṟṟaṉa eṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
irāmāyaṇak kāla mutal ilakkiyam eṉṟa peyarāl moḻi peyarppukaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil pukuttappaṭṭaṉa.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavi tamiḻmītum tamiḻarmītum āriyat tākkutalāl ēṟpaṭṭa māṟutalkaḷ. ivi palaviṭattum mikippaṭuttappaṭṭup pāmarat tamiḻ makkaḷ kātil naṉṟāka uṟikkumpaṭi eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, itaṉi oṭṭit tamiḻar tākkutalāl āriyam māṟupaṭṭirukkiṟatā eṉṟa kēḷvi eḻuvatumilli; eḻumāṉāl maṟikkavumpaṭukiṟatu.
vaṭamoḻi tamiḻmoḻi uṟavil vaṭamoḻiyiṉāltāṉ tamiḻmoḻiyil  māṟutalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻmoḻiyiṉāl vaṭamoḻiyil māṟutal ēṟpaṭa villi eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyiṉ paṇṭitarkaḷ tamiḻp paṇṭitarkaḷ mutaṟkoṇṭu eṇṇi varukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, moḻi, ilakkiyam, camayam, nākarikam, kali ākiya entat tuṟiyilum uṇmi itaṟku nērmāṟāṉa teṉpati varalāṟum, ārāycciyum varavara viḷakki varukiṉṟaṉa. moḻiyip paṟṟiya maṭṭil vaṭamoḻiyiṉāl tamiḻil ēṟpaṭṭa māṟutalkaḷ cila coṟkaḷiṉ varavu maṭṭumē. nāṭṭu ārvamum moḻiyārvamum mēmpaṭṭu varum kālaṅkaḷil ivi yellām eḷitil vilakkiviṭattakka cillaṟi māṟutalkaḷēyākum. āṉāl, tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ tākkiṉālum tamiḻiṉa moḻikaḷiṉ tākkiṉālum vaṭamoḻiyum vaṭa iṉa moḻikaḷum aṭintuḷḷa māṟutalkaḷ aṭippaṭiyāṉa viyum vilakkappaṭātaviyum ākum. ēṉeṉil, am māṟutalkaḷ veṟum col āṭci maṭṭilum aṉṟu. oli, iyalpu, colmuṟi, karuttut terivikkum muṟi ākiyavaṟṟiyum paṟṟiyatākum.
vaṭamoḻikku iṉamāṉa veḷināṭṭu moḻikaḷākiya pāracīkam, kirēkkam, ilattīṉam, jermāṉiya, kelṭiyak kuḻukkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil ḷa,ṇa,ṭa ākiya olikaḷ illi. vaṭamoḻiyil ivi vara vara mikutiyāki iṉṟiya vaṭa iṉa moḻikaḷil peritum malintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. immikuti tamiḻiṉatti yaṭuttup pēcappaṭum marāttiyilum, pīkāriyilum, vaṅkāḷiyilumē kūṭutalā yiruppatu kūrntu nōkkat takkatu.
vaṭamoḻiyil perumpālum ḷa,ṇa,ṭa ākiya ivvolikaḷ la, ṉa, ta ākiya eḻuttukaḷ tirintu  ēṟpaṭṭaviyē. āyiṉum, ivaṟṟiṉiṭiyēyum pala coṟkaḷ attakiya māṟutalkaḷukku iṭamillāta mutaṟ coṟkaḷākum. immutaṟ coṟkaḷil pala tamiḻ allatu tamiḻiṉac coṟkaḷē āyirukkak kūṭum. ṭa eṉṟa eḻuttu vakiyil pala coṟkaḷil ivvuṇmiyit teḷivākak kāṇalām. iraṇṭu eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ ivvakiyil taruvōm. kaṭam eṉpatu  kuṭam eṉṟa tamiḻc colliṉ iṉṉoru vaṭivam. itiviṭat teḷivākat tamiḻccol paṭṭaṇam eṉpatākum. iccol tamiḻil paṭṭiṉam eṉṟum paṭṭaṇam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. itaṉ uṇmiyāṉa poruḷ kaṭaṟ kariyiluḷḷa tuṟimuka nakaram eṉpatākum. tamiḻ nāṭṭil paṭṭaṇam eṉṟu muṭiyum peyarkaḷākiya ceṉṉip paṭṭaṇam, kāyal paṭṭaṇam, nākappaṭṭaṇam ākiyaviyum teluṅku nāṭṭiluḷḷa vicākappaṭṭaṇamkūṭak kaṭaṟkariyilēyē iruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. ulaka vaḻakkattil inta nuṭpatti makkaḷ kavaṉippatilli. vaṭamoḻiyāḷarum innuṭpam aṟiyāmal ataṉi nakaram eṉṟa poruḷilēyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. iccol paṭṭaṇam, paṭṭanam, pattanam eṉṟu palavāṟāka eḻutap peṟuvatum, vaṭamoḻi kāñci aracarkaḷāl vaḷarkkap paṭṭa kālattil vāḻnta taṇṭikkup piṟkālattilēyē itu vaṭamoḻiyil āḷap peṟuvatum atu putu varavu eṉpati valiyuṟuttum. mēlum paṭṭi (ūr) eṉpa tuṭaṉ iccol iṇivuṭiyatu.
ṇa eṉṟa eḻuttu varumiṭaṅkaḷil perumpālum nakaram māṟiyē ṇakara mākum. appaṭi māṟāta iṭaṅkaḷil ac coṟkaḷum, tamiḻ allatu tamiḻiṉac coṟkaḷēyā yirukkakkūṭum.
ivi tavira vēta kālattukkuppiṉ muṉ vaḻakku illāta pala coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil vantu kuvintaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ palavaṟṟi vaṭamoḻi uri yāciriyarkaḷē tēci allatu nāṭṭuc coṟkaḷ eṉṟu ottuk koṇṭaṉar. nām aṟintavari kuṟintatu mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉvari vaṭamoḻi innāṭṭil pēcappaṭṭatākat teriyavilli. ākavē, ataṉ coṟkaḷellām nūl vaḻakkilēyē pātukākkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. vētavaḻakku muḻuvatiyum yā°kar eṉpavar niruktam eṉṟa nūliṟ tokuttuk kūṟi yirukkiṟār. avarkkup piṟpaṭṭa pāṇiṉiyum tam kālattuc coṟkaḷi ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ col mūlaṅkaḷ allatu tātukkaḷit tokuttār. ivviraṇṭu tokuppukaḷuk kiṭiyilum putitāka vanta coṟkaḷilum pāṇiṉikkup piṉ kāñcipurattil vāḻnta  taṇṭi kālamvari vanta coṟ kaḷilum perumpālāṉavi tamiḻiṉac coṟkaḷē. “kāñci”k kālattukkup piṉ civa cittāntak karuttukaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil pukuntati yoṭṭip pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷum nēriṭiyākavō karuttu moḻi peyarppākavō vaṭa moḻiyil ceṉṟu ēṟiṉa. īcaṉ eṉpataṟkup patiyum āṉmā eṉpataṟku pacuvum maṟṟum māyi, pācam, malam mutaliya coṟkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyil kiyāḷap paṭṭaṉa.

pala coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil kalavāmal vaṭa iṉamoḻiyākiya intu° tāṉi mutaliyavaṟṟil vaḻaṅkiṉatuṇṭu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, tirumpu allatu vaḷi eṉṟa poruḷil intu°tāṉiyil vaḻaṅkum mōṭ (mōṭu) eṉṟa pakuti ivvakiyil tamiḻ muṭaṅku, muṭakku eṉpavaṟṟuṭaṉ iyiyu eṉṟu kāṇalām. vēṟu pala coṟkaḷ nēriṭiyāka vaṭa moḻikkuc cellāmal pāḷi, pākatam mutaliya nāṭṭu moḻikaḷiṉ vāyilāka vaṭamoḻi ceṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟi varum pakutikaḷil ārāya eṭuttuk koḷvōm.
coṟkaḷiṉ pōkkilum, ilakkaṇa amippilum, vācaka oḻuṅkilum, vaṭamoḻi tamiḻālaṭinta māṟṟam koñca nañcamaṉṟu. tamiḻil peyarccol orumiyilum, paṉmiyilum orē paṭiyāṉa urupu ēṟkum.  vaṭamoḻiyil teḷivāṉa urupu illāmal peyarc col citintu māṟum. āṉāl, nāḷaṭivil vaṭa moḻiyilum orumi, paṉmikaḷil orē paṭiyāṉa  urupuc coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. nāṉkām vēṟṟumi urupiṉiṭamāka kirutē (āka) eṉṟa collum aintām vēṟṟumi urupiṉiṭamāka ta° (iruntu) eṉṟa urupuc collum ittakiyavi. tamiḻiṉattiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl vaṭamoḻiyil muṉ urupukaḷ (ñacanayīṭiḷaviiṭin) aruki iṟutiyil oḻintaṉa; iṇi iṭip peyarkaḷ (cunaṭayaviena ñacaṭinṭiran) aruki viṉiyālaṇiyum peyar putitāka uṇṭu paṇṇappaṭṭatu. ummi iṭic collāl (ca) iṇittut toṭutta viṉi muṟṟukaḷiṉ iṭamāka viṉi eccaṅkaḷ peyar eccaṅkaḷ perukiṉa. col oḻuṅkilum paṇṭi vaṭa iṉa moḻiyākiya toṭuppu muṟi (hayēṭalaviu ḷunaḷūrananuna) nīṅkit tamiḻiṉa muṟiyākiya aṭukkumuṟi (ḷulavōnaviu yacacayapēnaanavē) ēṟpaṭṭatu.
ākavē, vaṭamoḻi tamiḻmoḻi ākiyavaṟṟiṉiṭiyil oru  collō paṇpō potuvākak kāṇappaṭṭāl uṭaṉ, tāṉē ilakkaṇak kottu  āciriyarākiya cuvāmināta tēcikarip pōla atu vaṭa moḻiyittāṉ cārntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu koḷvatu aṟivumākātu; maṉita nērmiyāṉa moḻip paṟṟumākātu eṉṟu kāṇalām. ārāycciyāḷar avviyaṟkiyāṉa moḻi uṇarccikkukkūṭa āḷākāmal uṇmiyi aṟiyum avāvi maṭṭumē kaṭippiṭittal vēṇṭum.
attakiya ārāyccikkut tūṇṭutal tarum vakiyil varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muntiya potuc coṟkaḷ, iṭikkālattil vaṭamoḻiyil ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyataṉāl vaṭa coṟkaḷeṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭa tamiḻc coṟkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟi iṅkē ārāya eṭuttukkoḷvōm.
ulakil ellā moḻikaḷukkum oru mutal tāy moḻi iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu pala nāṭṭumakkaḷum aṟiñarum karuti vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. innampikkiyāl potuppaṭa moḻiyārāyccikkup pala kuntakaṅkaḷ nērntirukkiṉṟaṉa. mēṉāṭṭār viviliya nūliṉ pēpal kōpura varalāṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu pallūḻikaḷukku muṉ yūtariṉ tāymoḻiyākiya ēpireyamē ulakat tāymoḻiyāyiruntatu eṉṟu nampi vantaṉar. āṉāl,  ārāycci innampikkikkuc caṟṟum  iṭam taravilli. vaṭanāṭṭār ulakiṉ tāymoḻi vaṭamoḻi eṉa nampiṉar. ōraḷavukku vaṭanāṭṭu moḻiyārāycciyum mēlināṭṭu moḻiyārāycciyum itaṟku utaviṉa. ēṉeṉil, vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷ yāvum ulakiṉ vēṟupala moḻikaḷum uṇmiyil orē āriya iṉamoḻikaḷātalāl peritum oppumi uṭiyaviyā yiruntaṉa. āṉāl, avviṉattukkum vaṭamoḻi tāy moḻiyalla; piṟa āriya iṉamoḻikaḷilum munti ilakkiya vaṭivam peṟṟa mutal moḻi vaṭamoḻiyē eṉṟu nāḷaṭivil teḷivāyiṟṟu.
teṉṉāṭṭil tamiḻmoḻi mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭu moḻikaḷip pōlavē ulakat tāymoḻi eṉṟu paḻantamiḻarāl koḷḷappaṭṭiruntatu. vaṭamoḻip paṟṟutalāl paṇṭitarkaḷ cila nāḷ itaṉi ēḷaṉam ceytu puṟakkaṇittaṉar. āṉāl, ārāycci itaṉi muṟṟilum ēṟkum nilimiyi lilliyāyiṉum itaṉi oruvāṟu viḷakkum pala cāṉṟukaḷ tarukiṉṟatu. aṟiñar kālṭuvel tamiḻiṉattiṉ iṉṟiya moḻikaḷākiya tamiḻ, maliyāḷam, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷu mutaliya paṇpaṭṭa perumoḻikaḷiṭiyēyum paṇpaṭāta pala moḻikaḷiṭiyēyum  piṟamoḻikaḷ allatu moḻi uṭkuḻukkaḷ oṉṟi oṉṟu taḻuvuvativiṭat tamiḻ  avi ovvoṉṟiyum taṉittaṉiyāka ovvoru vakiyil taḻuvi niṟkum naṭunāyaka iṇippu moḻiyāyirukkiṟatu eṉṟu viḷakkiṉār. atumaṭṭumaṉṟu ulakiṉ piṟa iṉaṅkaḷākiya āriya iṉam, cemittiya iṉam, maṅkōliya iṉam, ā°tirika iṉam, āppirikka - amerikka iṉaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉum, avaṟṟiṉ taṉi uṟuppukaḷuṭaṉum tamiḻ paṟpala oṟṟumikaḷ uṭiyatu eṉpati aṟiñarkaḷ palar eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. ittaṉi moḻikaḷ, ittaṉi iṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻ piṉṉikkiṭakka avi, tammuḷ ivvaḷavu piṉṉalkaḷukkum iṭiyē tamiḻuṭaṉ aṭippaṭit toṭarpu koṇṭu kiṭappatik kāṇa, tamiḻ ulakat tāymoḻiyāy irāviṭiṉum iṉṟu attāy moḻikku mikavum arukilirukkum talimoḻiyum ellā moḻi kaḷiṉ iṇippu moḻiyum ākum eṉṟu aṟiñar kālṭuvel mutaliyōr kūṟiṉar.
iṟṟikkup pallāyiram, allatu paṉṉūṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉiruntē ēṟpaṭṭa ivvuṟavukaḷil cilavē, cilacamayam tamiḻiyum vaṭamoḻiyiyum, cila camayam tamiḻiyum āriyap potu moḻikaḷiyum, cila camayam tamiḻiyum vaṭamoḻi nīṅkalāṉa piṟa āriya iṉa moḻikaḷiyum iṇikkiṉṟaṉa.  tamiḻukkum āriya iṉa muḻumikkum potumiyāṉa paṇpukku ukarac cuṭṭi eṭuttukkāṭṭākak kūṟamuṭiyum. itu tamiḻil aṇmiyum cēymiyum allāta ellā uṟavukaḷiyum-iṭippaṭṭa toli, piṉpuṟam, mēl, kīḻ, uḷ, maṟinta nuṇporuḷ ākiya aṉittiyum kuṟikkum. vaṭamoḻiyilum āriya iṉattilum uccam, upari, ut-. mutaliya coṟkaḷilum, coṟ pakutikaḷilum itu mēl eṉṟa poruḷil vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.
tamiḻ paḻiya, putu eṉṟa coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṇavilli. āṉāl, paṇṭiya āriya iṉa moḻiyākiya kirēkka moḻiyil uḷḷaṉa. (āṅkilattil ñayaṭayanaṭiṭaṭipala paḻimiyārāycci eṉṟa col itiliruntu  vantatākum.) iṉṉum  arici,  nāvāy mutaliya coṟkaḷ (kirēkka moḻiyiṉ oricā, nāviyā) ittakiya paḻaṅkāla uṟavukaḷ alla eṉṟum alek sāṇṭar paṭiyeṭuppiṉ piṉ ēṟpaṭṭa kirēkkar-tamiḻar vāṇipa uṟavāleṉ ṟum koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. eppaṭiyum paḻiya, putu pōṉṟa coṟkaḷ ivvāṟu ēṟpaṭṭirukka muṭiyātu. ākavē, āriyarāṉa kirēkkar kirēkka nilam varumuṉ aṇmi mēṉāṭukaḷil paraviyirunta ījiya vakuppiṉar tamiḻaruṭaṉ koṇṭa toṭarpiṉ payaṉākavē ivvuṟavukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām.
aracar, iṭiyar (allatu pacu mēyppavar) eṉṟu irumippaṭṭa poruḷil ‘kō’ eṉṟa collum ‘ār’ (niṟivu) eṉṟa col mūlattiṉuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiya āriyaṉ (āṉṟōṉ) eṉṟa collum vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻkkum potuvāṉa ittakiya mutal uṟavuc coṟkaḷām. cuṟṟu, cūḻ eṉpavaṟṟuṭaṉ uṟavuṭiya cuṉṉam, cūriyaṉ eṉpaviyum ivaṟṟiṉpāṟpaṭum.
eṉavē tamiḻkkum vaṭamoḻikkum potuvāṉa coṟkaḷiṉ oru pakuti maṉita nākarikat toṭakkak kālattil ellā moḻikaḷukkum tāyakamāka oru tamiḻiṉa moḻi irunta kālattic cārntateṉpatum, avvoru moḻiyiṉ nērvaḻi moḻiyāy iṉṟu iyaṅkuvatu tamiḻeṉpatum, attakiya tamiḻuṭaṉ ippaṇṭiya uṟavu koṇṭa moḻi vaṭamoḻi maṭṭumalla, piṟa āriya iṉa moḻikaḷum, piṟa iṉa moḻikaḷum kūṭavē eṉpatum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
ip paḻaṅkāla uṟaviviṭṭu varalāṟṟuk kālattil teṉ moḻiyi liruntu vaṭamoḻiyāḷar eṭuttuk koṇṭa coṟkaḷil tamiḻc col eṉṟu iṉṟu kāṭṭattakum coṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟi muṟippaṭi iṉi ārāyvōm.
tamiḻukkum vaṭamoḻikkum potuvāṉa coṟkaḷ ellām vaṭa coṟkaḷ eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭuvatu tavaṟu eṉṟu muṉṉiya pakutikaḷil kāṭṭiya ceytikaḷ teḷivu paṭuttum, piṟanāṭṭu vaṭa iṉamoḻikaḷil kāṇāmal vētakāla vaṭamoḻiyil maṭṭum kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷ, vētakāla vaṭamoḻiyil kāṇāmal piṟkāla vaṭamoḻiyil kāṇappaṭum pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ, vaṭamoḻiyil kāṇāmal pāḷi, pākatam mutaliya paṇṭip pēccu moḻikaḷilum inti, vaṅkāḷi, kujarātti, marātti mutaliya taṟkālap pēccu moḻikaḷilum kāṇappaṭum kaṇakkaṟṟa coṟkaḷil perumpakuti ākiyavi. tamiḻiliruntu nērākavō tamiḻiṉa moḻikaḷiliruntō eṭukkappaṭṭu vaṭamoḻiyiyaṟkippaṭi māṟiṉa coṟkaḷē yākum. iṅṅaṉam māṟiya māṟṟam iruvakippaṭum. oṉṟu colliṉ poruḷi moḻi peyarttal, maṟikkāṭu eṉpataṟku vētāraṇyam eṉṟum, tirumali eṉpataṟku srīparvatam eṉṟum moḻi peyarttuk koḷvatu ivvaki. iṉṉoruvaki tamiḻ olikaḷi appaṭiyē vittum tirittum vaḻaṅkal, potiki mali vaṭa moḻiyil malayam eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruppatu tiripu mikuti peṟāta vaḻakku. kāviri eṉṟa tamiḻaka āṟṟiṉ peyar kāvēri eṉṟu māṟupaṭṭatum viki vēkavati eṉṟu māṟupaṭṭatum palavakiyil oliyi māṟṟit tirittuk koṇṭa tiripu vaḻakkukaḷ. ittakiya coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil ceṉṟaṉa eṉpati nili nāṭṭac cila cāṉṟukaḷ coṟkaḷit taruvōm.
akil ulakil iṉṟukūṭa vēṟeṅkum viḷiyātu potiki mali yaṭivāram oṉṟilēyē viḷiyum oru vakic cantaṉamām. itu kuḻampāy arippataṟku maṭṭumaṉṟic cāmpirāṇi pōl pukippataṟkum vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatu. peṇkaḷ akiṟpukiyi ūṭṭit talimayirkku maṇamūṭṭuvar. tamiḻilakkiyat tilum vaṭamoḻi ilakkiyattilum itu peritum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu vaḷarvatu potiki yileṉṟu vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻi yil itaṉ peyar akaru eṉpatākum. teṉṉāṭṭu maliyiṉ poruḷ teṉ moḻiyiliruntu mutalil tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻiyil ciṟitu māṟutaluṭaṉ itu kiyāḷappaṭṭatu.
yāṉi intiyāvil mēṟkut toṭar malippuṟattilum parmāvilum āppirikkāvilum maṭṭumē kāṇappaṭum vilaṅku. itaṟkuc cariyāṉa iṭukuṟip peyar vaṭamoḻiyil kiṭiyātu. kari, ha°ti mutaliya peyarkaḷellām kiyuṭiya vilaṅku eṉṟu poruḷtarum iṭukuṟik kāraṇap peyarkaḷēyākum. iṉṟum yāṉi paḻakkum pākar perumpālum tamiḻnāṭṭi yaṭutta maliyāḷa nāṭṭiṉarē. āṉāl, yāṉinūl iṉṟum caṅka kālattilumkūṭa vaṭamoḻi yilēyē iruntatām. innūlum piṟa nūlkaḷum tamiḻiṉiṉṟutāṉ vaṭa moḻikkuc ceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭumeṉak koḷḷutal iyalputāṉē. aṅṅaṉam collumpōtu avaṟṟiṉ coṟkaḷ pala mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi nērākavum moḻi peyarttum tirittum vaṭamoḻi pukuntaṉa eṉpatum, paḻiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻil aḻikkappaṭṭa piṉ avi vaṭa moḻikku urimiyākkap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟum kūṟutal vēṇṭum.
civa camayam iṉṉum muṟkālattum tamiḻnāṭu maṭṭilumaṉṟi intiyā eṅkum parantirunta teṉpatu yāvarum aṟintatoṉṟē. āyiṉum ārāycci muṟiyil civa cintāntam nilipeṟṟu vaḷarcci yaṭintatu teṉṉāṭṭil tāṉ. ataṉ nūlkaḷ vaṭa moḻiyiliruntu tamiḻukku moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu aṇmivarip palar kūṟivantaṉar. civap peruntalivarum ārāycci aṟiñarumāṉa aruḷtiru maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ annūlkaḷ meykaṇṭār mutaliya tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷāl tamiḻil ceyyappaṭṭati vaṭa moḻip paṟṟāḷar cilar vaṭamoḻiyil piṉ moḻipeyarttuk koṇṭaṉar eṉpatik kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. mēlum iv vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi nūlkaḷukku neṭunāḷ muṉṉarē tamiḻ nāṭṭil civa cittānta uṇmikaḷ naṉku paraviyiruntaṉa eṉpati 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭilumuḷḷa cōḻar pallavar kalveṭṭukaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutap peṟṟatākak koḷḷappaṭum tirumūlar tirumantiramum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum. vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ceṉṟa ic civa cittāntattuṭaṉum ataṉ kiḷikaḷāṉa caṅkarar māyāvātattuṭaṉum, irāmāṉujar, mattuvar, nīlakaṇṭar mutaliyavar neṟikaḷuṭaṉum pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil payilalāyiṉa. avaṟṟi mēlē vivarittuk kūṟuvōm.
periya purāṇak katikaḷ, tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇak katikaḷ, tamiḻar katikaḷ ākiya yāvum vaṭa moḻiyiliruntu moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyiṉ moḻipeyarppukaḷi mūlamākak koṇṭu kūṟuvatu evvaḷavu pētimi?
potuppaṭa ivvilakkiya nūlkaḷellām vaṭamoḻikkup putu varavu eṉṟu kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ ārāypavarkkuk kiṭṭātu pōkavilli. nāyaṉ mārkaḷ, āḻvārkaḷ peyarkaḷ cila camayam pōtiya tamiḻaṟivillāta vaṭamoḻip pulavarāl tavaṟāka moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāratattil pāṇṭiyaṉ tali nakar maṇavūr eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. uṇmiyil avvūriṉ peyar maṇalūr ākum. tamiḻil ēṭṭeḻuttil lakaram vakaramākak kāṇappaṭṭatu. moḻi peyarppavar tamiḻaṟiñarāyiruntāl ittakiya ciṟu tavaṟukaḷukku iṭamirātu.
iṉṉumoru ciṟu cuvitarum uṇmi ilakkiya aṟivu teṟkiṉiṉ ṟum vaṭakkē ceṉṟateṉpati valiyuṟuttum. tamiḻil ceyaṟpāṭṭuviṉi paṭu, peṟu, uṇ eṉṟa mūṉṟu pakutikaḷiṉ cērkkiyāl peṟappaṭum. aṭikkap paṭṭāṉ, aṭikkappeṟṟāṉ, aṭiyuṇṭāṉ ellām orē poruḷ. nōyvāyp paṭuvatiyum itēpōl nōyuṇṭāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakkam. tamiḻar ceṭikaḷiṉ nōykkup putumiyāṉa peyar koṭuttuḷḷaṉar. tēṅkāyiṉ nōy tēri: viḷāṅkāyiṉ nōy āṉi: toṭarāka varumpōtu tēriyuṇṭa tēṅkāy eṉṟu varuvataṉāl tēri eṉṟa uyiriṉam tēṅkāyi uṇṭatu eṉṟa mayakkam ēṟpaṭukiṟatu.  paṇṭit tamiḻar itu veṟum mayakkam eṉpati aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpati nālaṭiyāril “tēṅkāyuṇṇāta tērikku uṇṭa kuṟṟam ēṟpaṭuvatupōla” eṉa varum kuṟippiṉāl  kāṇalām. vaṭamoḻi yāḷarum avarkaḷip piṉpaṟṟiya ellāp piṟa moḻiyāḷarum tamiḻiṉ it taṉicciṟappu aṟiyātu ataṉi moḻipeyarttu ulakilillāta oru ceytiyi uḷḷatākak karuti mayaṅkiṉar.
tamiḻariṉ paṇṭiya varaṉmuṟi urimi tāyvaḻiyil eṉpatu aṟiñar muṭivu. iṉṟu vari atu maliyāḷattilum paḻantamiḻ nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭirunta vaṅkakkaṭal tīvukaḷilum nilipeṟṟuḷḷatu. iṉṟu poruḷ varaṉmuṟi urimi  kuṟikkum tāyapākam eṉṟa collil mutaṟ pakutiyākiya tāyam-ippaḻiya urimiyiṉi niṉivūṭṭuvatu. tāyvaḻi varavē tāyam eṉappaṭṭatu. itu iṉṟu colliṉ mutaṟ poruḷiḻantu vaṭamoḻiyil kuruṭṭuttaṉamāka vaḻaṅkap peṟukiṟatu.
pūci, pūci eṉṟa coṟkaḷ pūk koṇṭu  ceyvatu, pūcuvatu eṉṟa kuṟippuṭiyavi eṉpar aṟiñar  kālṭuvel. innūl vēta moḻiyilillā vaḻakku. piṟa āriya iṉamoḻikaḷilum illi eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
māyi eṉpatu  ulakat tattuvattik kuṟikkum col. toṭakkattil ulakam aḻivatu  allatu tirivatu eṉṟa uṇmi tōṉṟa māy-eṉṟa pakutiyi liruntu māyi, māyki eṉṟa coṟkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. (iṉṟum itaṉi māyi, māyki eṉṟu eḻutuvatu  uṇṭu) māyāvātikaḷ itaṉip peru vaḻakkāka vaḻaṅki vaṭamoḻikkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. māy eṉṟa pakutiyuṭaṉēyē toṭarpuṭiya iṉṉum iru pakutikaḷ mā, māl eṉpaṉavām. ivi karumi,  mayakkam eṉṟum poruḷ tarum. tirumāl māyā uruviṉaṉ eṉṟu pāṭap paṭuvatum, kariya uruviṉaṉ eṉpatum, umi vaṭiviṉaṉ eṉappaṭuvatum cērttu nōkkiṉāl camaya aṭippaṭik karuttuṭiya ivvoppu mikaḷ aṉittum tamiḻar karuttiṉiṉṟum tamiḻ moḻiyiṉiṉṟumē tōṟṟiṉa eṉpatu viḷaṅkum.
uṭalil 72000 nāṭikaḷuḷ 10 talimiyuṭiṉa eṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷḷum ciṟappuṭiyavi. iṭikali, piṉkali, cuḻimuṉi eṉṟum ellāvaṟṟukkum naṭunāyakamāṉatu cuḻimuṉi  eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ cuḻimuṉi untic cuḻimutal neṟṟimuṉi vari celvatu.  unticcuḻi piṟappukkuk kāraṇamākiya aṟituyil niliyiyum, neṟṟimuṉi piṟappaṟukkak kāraṇamāṉa meyyaṟi viyum uṇarttum. eṉavē cuḻimuṉi eṉpatiluḷḷa cuḻi-etaṉi uṇarttum eṉṟu kaṇṭōm. ic cuḻiyiṉ vaṭivattiyē ōṅkāram eṉṟum piḷḷiyār cuḻi eṉṟum koḷkiṟōm. mēlum aṟituyil cuḻutti eṉappaṭum. vaṭamoḻi yāḷar cuḻi muṉiyi suṣīmnā eṉṟum cuḻuttiyi suṣīpriti eṉṟum tirittukkoṇṭaṉar. puttakattit tiruṭik koṇṭu pōṉavaṉukku  ati aṟiyum aṟiviyumā tiruṭikkoṇṭu pōka muṭiyum? vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ‘īyaṭittāṉ kāppi’kaḷiṭiyē coṟkaḷiṉ uyirkaḷ pōyviṭṭaṉa.
neṭuṅkālamākat tamiḻarum intiyāvil piṟa tāy moḻikaḷ pēcum makkaḷum tāymoḻiyilum intiyap paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉamāṉa tamiḻiṉattilum (nāṉkām aintām varuṇattavariṭattilum) paṟṟutaliṉṟi avviṉa moḻi kaḷip pēṇātu viṭṭataṉ payaṉāka avaṟṟiṉ coṟkaḷ evi, vaṭa coṟkaḷ evi eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu terivataṟkiṭamillāma liruntatu anniliyil vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tam ilakkiyattil oru colli vaḻaṅkiviṭṭāl āḷillāta iṭattil akappaṭṭa tellām uṭimi tām eṉpatupōl  atu  avarkaḷuṭi teṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭatu. pāṇiṉi pōṉṟa ilakkaṇa aṟiñar coṟkaḷi ārāyntu col mūlaṅkaḷ tokuppukaḷ kaṇṭatupōl piṟamoḻikaṭku yārum tokuppuc ceyyavilli. tamiḻil tolkāppiyar  kālammutal moḻi ilakkaṇam ēṟpaṭṭapōtilum  coltokuppu mikap piṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē ēṟpaṭṭatu. appōtum ilakkiya vaḻakkukku veḷiyēyuḷḷa ulakavaḻakkuc coṟkaḷi yārum tokukka muṉ varavilli. in nāḷvari evarum eṇṇavumilli. ilakkiyamum camaya caccaravukaḷāl perum pakutikaḷum  citintu oru pakutiyē namakku vantu cērntuḷḷataṉāl ārāycci ceyvōr. neṭuṅkālam vaṭamoḻiyāḷar colliyē mantira moḻiyākak koṇṭu mayaṅkiṉar.
yām tamiḻeṉak koḷḷum coṟkaḷ iṉṉum palavaṟṟi emakkut tōṉṟiya kāraṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ mēlē ārāyvōm. tamiḻar orucārpi-atilum tāymoḻik ketirāṉa orucārpi akaṟṟi nērmiyāka muṉ muṭivu etuvumiṉṟi ārāya oruppaṭa ivi utavumāyiṉ em muyaṟci vīṇ muyaṟciyaṉṟu eṉṟa niṟivu aṭivōm.
ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉṟa coṟkaḷ vaṭa coṟkaḷ eṉṟum lakṣiyam, lakṣaṇam eṉpaviyē avaṟṟiṉ vaṭamoḻi uruvaṅkaḷ eṉṟum palarāl koḷḷappaṭṭa kālam uṇṭu. iṉṟum palar appōli vātatti nampakkūṭum. ākavē, avi vaṭacoṟkaḷ alla eṉṟu nām koḷvataṟkāṉa kāraṇaṅkaḷi iṅkē viḷakkuvōm.
tamiḻil ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉṟa coṟkaḷ avi iṉṟu vaḻaṅkum poruḷkaḷilēyē varalāṟṟuk keṭṭiya kālantoṭṭu vaḻaṅki vantiruk kiṉṟaṉa. ikkaruttukaḷi viḷakka vēṟu coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkiyatākavum teriya villi. (iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṟa mūṉṟil iyal eṉpati ilakkiyam eṉac cilar tavaṟākak koḷkiṉṟaṉar. iyalil ilakkaṇam oru pakuti eṉpatiyum ici, nāṭakam ākiyavaṟṟuḷ ilakkiyamum uṇṭu, ilakkaṇamum uṇṭu eṉpatiyum kavaṉittal vēṇṭum.)
tamiḻil iccoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu vantaṉa veṉṟāl iṅkē muṉ vēṟu col iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamillātatāl ic coṟkaḷ tamiḻil  vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷē. iṉṉum, vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu ic coṟkaḷ vantaṉa eṉṟu niliniṟutta virumpuvōr mutalil accoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkiṉavā vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉavā eṉṟu pārttal vēṇṭum. vaṭa moḻiyil aṉṟum iṉṟum ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉṟa coṟkaḷiṉiṭamāka lakṣiyam, lakṣaṇam eṉṟa coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatēyilli. cāhityam, vyā karṇam eṉṟa coṟkaḷē avaṟṟik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
appaṭiyāyiṉ ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ pakuti etu? ilakku eṉpatēyām. itaṉi iṉṟum tamiḻ nāṭṭil iṭam eṉṟa poruḷil ceṭṭināṭṭār vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉar. ilaku, ilaṅku eṉpaṉa itaṉ mūlap pakutikaḷ. cellumiṭam eṉṟa poruḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭup piṉ kuṟikkōḷ, karut tiyal  kuṟikkōḷ, poruḷkaḷil iccol vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. kaṭṭu eṉṟa colliliruntu kaṭṭiyam (kōmāḷittaṉam) amintatu pōl ilakku eṉpati liruntu kuṟippu viṉi yaṭiyākap  piṟanta am īṟṟup paṇpuppeyarē ilakkiyam ākum.
ilakkaṇam eṉpatu ilakku eṉṟa pakutiyuṭaṉ vaṇṇam eṉṟa colliṉ mūlamākiya aṇam eṉṟa paṇpuvikuti cērnta col. iṉṉaṇam eṉṟa toṭaril itu muḻuc collāy varuvatu kāṇka.
vāḻkkik kuṟikkōḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭum uṟuti  nūlkaḷ ilakkiyam. avaṟṟiṉ paṇpukaḷi ārāyvatu  ilakkaṇam. ivvaḷavu teḷḷiya uyariya kalippaṇpuṭiya coṟkaḷ ilakkiyattukkum ilakkaṇat tukkum entap putiyamoḻiyilum paḻammoḻiyilum illi. uṇmiyil piṟamoḻikaḷil ivaṟṟukkāṉa coṟkaḷiṉ mutaṟporuḷ nakippukkiṭamāvaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyiṉ ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉpavaṟṟukkuc cariyāṉa coṟkaḷiṉ mutaṟporuḷ uṭaṉiruttal, viḷakkam eṉpaṉavām. āṅkilam mutaliya mēṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷil ilakkiyam eṉpatu eḻutappaṭuva teṉṟum, ilakkaṇam eṉpatu coṟpayiṟci eṉṟumē  poruḷ paṭukiṉṟaṉa. collāṭciyil maṭṭumaṉṟip  poruḷāṭciyilum ittakiya uyarvuṭiya tamiḻar  celvak kuvikaḷi avaṟṟi eṉṟumaṟiyā mūṅki moḻiyākiya vaṭamoḻiyiṉ mītu cumattal eṉṉa aṟiyāmi!
ivviru coṟkaḷōṭu toṭarpuṭiya col ilakkam eṉpatu, pattum nūṟum eṇṇalaḷavāyirunta kālam tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ piriyāta kālam eṉa eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉpiṉ orupaṭi kūṭṭi oṉpatu nūṟṟukkup piṉ pattu nūṟu ākamuṭiyum eṉṟa poruḷpaṭa ‘āy iṟum’ allatu ‘āyiṟum’ eṉṟa toṭarē āyiramāyiṟṟu. piṉṉum kaṇakku vaḷarcciyum kaṇakkar vaḷarcciyum ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ kiṭṭattaṭṭa atē poruḷil ilakkam, kōṭi eṉṟa poruḷ kaḷiyum amittaṉar. ilakkam eṉpatu  ilakku āyatu eṉpatu, iṉṉum ciṟu celvar āyirattiyum, patiṉāyirattiyum peruñ celvar āka virumpu vōr mutaṟkaṇ ilakka (nūṟāyira) ttiyē ilakkākak koḷḷutal kāṇalām. kōṭi oru taṉimaṉitaṉ celva elliyik kāṭṭiṟṟu. kōṭi vakuttavar  koṭi kaṭṭi vāḻntaṉar. akkoṭi kōṭiyik kuṟitta collē yātalum koṭinili, koṭukku ākiya coṟkaḷil atu ap poruḷil varutalum kāṇalām.
koṭi, kōṭi ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭa pakuti kōṭu-kōṇu eṉpatām. itaṉaṭiyākap piṟanta iraṇṭu coṟkaḷ kōṇam eṉpatum kōṭṭi eṉpatum ākum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu vaṭamoḻiyil maṭṭumaṉṟi āriyamoḻikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷḷum kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. iraṇṭāvatu vaṭa moḻiyil arukalākavē vaḻaṅkukiṟatāyiṉum kaṉṉaṭa, marāṭṭiya nāṭukaḷil peruvaḻakkamāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. varalāṟṟu māṇavar, perum pōr nikaḻnta iṭamākiya talik kōṭṭi yiyum vaṭintiyat taṉiyaracākiya irāvu kōṭṭiyiyum kavaṉikka.
viciyar eṉṟa collum karuttum vakuppum vaṭanāṭṭic cārntaṉa. ataṟkiṇiyākak koḷḷappaṭum collākiya vaṇikar eṉṟa collum poruḷum vakuppum tamiḻ nāṭṭic cērntavi. vaṇikar eṉṟa col vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkappaṭiṉum atu vaṭacol aṉṟu, tamiḻc collēyām eṉṟu kāṭṭuvōm.
āriyar intiyāvukku varumpōtum neṭunāḷ piṉṉarum, vāṇikam ceytilar. mēlum kaṭal kaṭanta vāṇikam paṇṭum iṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭiṉari viṭat teṉṉāṭṭiṉarālēyē mikutiyum payiṉṟu varappeṟukiṟatu. pāṇṭiyaṉ cōḻaṉ āṭciyil kaṭaṟṟuṟip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ paṟṟik kēḷvippaṭuvatu pōl vaṭanāṭṭil eṉṟum (mukalāyar āṭciyil kūṭa) nām kēḷvippaṭavilli. eṉavē vaṇika vakupput tōṉṟi vaḷarnta pakuti tamiḻ nāṭēyātalāl accol tamiḻēyātal vēṇṭum eṉka.
vaṇikar eṉṟa colliṉ pakuti vaṇmi eṉpatu. vēḷāḷar nilac celvamikkavar, viruntōmpuvar. vaṇikar kalac celvamikkavar, vaṇmiyāl vaḻaṅkupavar.
tamiḻmakkaḷiṭiyē vaṇikar ceḻittōṅkiyiruntataṟku irukku vētamē cāṉṟu pakarum. tamiḻarākiya ta°yūkkaḷ annāḷ nakarkaḷum araṇkaḷum tuṟimukaṅkaḷum uṭiyavarāyintaṉar eṉṟum, avarkaḷiṭiyē vaṇikar paṇikar eṉṟa vakiyiṉar iruntaṉar eṉṟum irukkuvētam urikkiṟatu. ivviṭattil paṇi eṉṟa vaṭacolliṉ pakaram tamiḻukkup potuvāṉa valleḻuttup pakaramēyaṉṟi melinta pakaram aṉṟu. valleḻuttup pakaramum vakaramum tamiḻil pōliyāka māṟum. vaṭamoḻiyil meṉ pakaram aṅṅaṉam māṟum. ākavē muṟkālattil paṇi, paṇikar eṉṟum piṉ vaṇikar eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ tamiḻiṉ kiḷic coṟkaḷēyām eṉka.
iṉṟum maliyāḷattil kittoḻil mutalāḷi vakuppiṉar paṇikkar eṉappaṭutal kāṇka.
nīr, mīṉ, kali, niliyam ākiya coṟkaḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟu vaṭamoḻi pukkateṉat teḷivāka viḷaṅkum coṟkaḷeṉak kālṭuvel kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟiṉ pakuti nīrmi, miṉṉutal, kal (kaṟṟal),nil eṉpaṉavām. aṉṟiyum ivvakiyic cērnta tamiḻc collām ataṉuṭaṉotta puṉal tamiḻc collātalum ataṉuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiya kaṉal, kaṉalutal ākiyavaṟṟiyum kāṇka.
mēṟ kūṟiya coṟkaḷuṭaṉ cērtteṇṇattakka āriya tamiḻc col valayam ākum. val eṉṟa pakuti āṟṟaliyum valam āṟṟaluṭiya valakki yiyum ataṉāl paṭum veṟṟiyiyum koṭiyiyum kuṟikkum. cuṟṟi varupavar valappuṟamāka varuvatāl valam cuṟṟutal eṉṟum valayam cuṟṟu eṉṟum āyiṟṟu. itaṉ iṉṉoru vaṭivam vaḷiyam. uṇmiyil itu vaḷi eṉṟa pakutiyiliruntu vantatu. vaṭamoḻiyil ipporuṭ ciṟappu kaḷiṉṟi ippeyar iṭu kuṟiyāka eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. (veṟṟiyik kuṟikkum caṅkiṉ vāy valappuṟamāyaminta tātaliṉ atu valampuriyāyiṟṟu).
naṭa eṉpatiliruntu naṭaṉamum, nāṭu eṉpatiliruntu nāṭakamum nāṭṭiyamum tōṉṟiṉa. vaṭamoḻiyil icciṟappukaḷ illātatōṭu ellām naṭaṉam eṉṟa potup poruḷuṭaṉēyē vaḻaṅkiṉa.
tāṇṭavam orukāl orukālmītu tāṇṭi niṟkum nilikuṟikkum taṇṭam, taṭi eṉṟa colluṭaṉ iyipuṭiyatu. ataṉāl aṭittut taṇṭiyatē taṇṭaṉiyiṉ mutaṟpaṭi ātalāl taṇṭaṉi eṉṟa collum, āṟṟaluṭiyavar pātukāppil ceṉṟu paṇintu poruḷ tarutal taṇṭam vaḻaṅkal eṉṟum, pātukāppil oruvaṉ taṉṉi oppuvittal taṇṭaṉiṭuvateṉṟum vaḻaṅkiyatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
pacu, pācam, pacumi, paci eṉṟa coṟkaḷum nayam, naci eṉṟa coṟkaḷum nayappu, nippu (nippāci) ney, necavu eṉpavaṟṟuṭaṉ oppiṭṭu nōkkattakka tamiḻc coṟkaḷ. vaṭamoḻiyil avaṟṟukkup pakutiyilli.
ivaṟṟippōlavē vīram eṉṟa col vīṟuṭaṉum, tiṭam eṉpatu tiṭpam, tiṭṭam, tiṇṇam eṉpavaṟṟuṭaṉum māraṇam, māṟu eṉpataṉuṭaṉum, kēḷikki, kēli, kēḷ, kēḷmi eṉpavaṟṟuṭaṉum nāṉā (vaki) eṉpatu (nālā) pakkam eṉpataṉuṭaṉum  uṟavuṭiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ.
pālaṉ pālkuṭi māṟāc ciṟu paruvattiṉaṉ eṉṟa karuttil eḻuntatu. vāl eṉpatu veṇmi. tūymip poruḷuṭiyatu. iḷaṅkuruttik kuṟippākakkoṇṭu iḷami kuṟittalāl vālipaṉ iḷiñaṉukkēṟṟa collāyiṟṟu.
tuṇṭam eṉṟa col tuṇṭu eṉṟa tamiḻc collēyām. tuṇi eṉṟa viṉiyuṭaṉ uṟavuṭiyatu. itēpōl piṇṭamum tamiḻccollēyām. piḷavu paṭṭa tuṇṭē piṇṭam. itaṟku etirccollāṉa aṇṭam, aḷittu eṉṟa pakutiyuṭaṉ iyipuṭiya tamiḻc col ākum.
vaṭamoḻiyil cinti, kavali ārāycci ivviraṇṭiyum kuṟikkum, tamiḻil ārāycciyik kuṟikkum cintutal, citaṟutal ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ itu toṭarpuṭiyatu. ceytiyip pirittu aṟiyum aṟivi itu kuṟippatāl nalla tamiḻc collēyākum.
cāram, cāṟu eṉṟa colluṭaṉum vākku, vācakam vāy eṉṟa colluṭa ṉum cārpu koṇṭavi. kalayam kalattiṉ piṟitoru vaṭivam. kalakam kalavaram eṉṟa tamiḻc collip pōl kala eṉṟa pakutiyaṭiyākap piṟantatu.
ku eṉpatu vaḷivu eṉṟa karuttuṭiya pakuti. vaṭamoḻi yilum iv viṭic col ipporuḷil vaḻaṅkukiṟatāyiṉum atu arukalāṉa vaḻakkē. ataṉi oṭṭiya coṟkaḷ tamiḻil peruvāriyāṉavi; colliṉ iṉṟi yamiyāp pakutiyāṉavi. vaṭamoḻiyil ivviṭi eṉṟum taṉittuc col lukkup puṟa aṭiyākavē niṟkiṟatu. (e-ṭu) rūpam ku-rūpam; tarkkam ku-tarkkam eṉpaṉapōla.  tamiḻil kuvi, kuvi. kuṭam. kuli, kuppam, kuvaṭu, kum mutal ākiya coṟkaḷil pirikka muṭiyā aḷavu colluṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ākavē, ivviṭic collākiya colmūlam tamiḻē eṉṟal teḷivu.
im mūlattiṉ tiripukaḷuḷ oṉṟē kuki eṉpatu. itaṉ iṉṉoru vaṭivam kuvi. aṟukam pullukku vaṭamoḻi yiluḷḷa peyarākiya kuci eṉpatukūṭa itaṉuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiya tamiḻc  collēyāyirukkakkūṭum. ‘ku’ eṉṉum iṭic colliṉ neṭiluruvattiliruntu kūrmi, kūṭu, kūvu mutaliya coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. iccoṟkaḷuṭaṉ iṇippu uṭiyatē kūval eṉpatu.  itaṉ tiripu kūvam, kūpam eṉṟām. iṟuti vaṭivamē vaṭamoḻiyil kāṇappaṭu vatu. vaṭamoḻi pukka tamiḻc coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟākavē itaṉik koḷḷalām.
tamiḻ allatu tirāviṭamoḻic cārpuṭiyatākak koḷḷakkūṭiya iṉṉoru col cūṭāmaṇi, āpātacūṭam eṉṟa vaṭacoṟṟoṭarkaḷil varum cūṭā eṉṟa coṟkaḷ ākum. vaṭamoḻiyil itaṉ poruḷ tali eṉpatākum. tamiḻil taliyil aṇi eṉṟa poruḷuṭiya cūṭu eṉṟa collum, teluṅkil mukaṅkoṇṭu pār eṉṟa  poruḷil varum cūṭu eṉṟa collum, itaṉuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyaṉa. cuṭiki (pāmpiṉ paṭamum) itaṉic cārnta iṉṉoru col, tamiḻil tali eṉṟa poruḷil itu vaḻakkiliruntirukkak kūṭum. vaṭamoḻiyil itu piṟkāla vaḻakkēyātalālum itaṉ cārpuc coṟkaḷāṉa tamiḻc coṟkaḷum peru vaḻakkāyt  toṉṟutoṭṭu  vaḻaṅki varuvatālum vaṭamoḻikkiṉamāṉa piṟa moḻikaḷil itaṉ cārpāṉa coṟkaḷiṉmiyālum itu tirāviṭac collēyāmeṉat teḷiyalām.
kālam, kāl (oḷi vīcu) eṉṟa mūlatti uṭiyatu. oḷivīcum katiravaṟ kāki ataṉ mūlam teriyappaṭum kālattukkāyiṟṟu. kāl kāṟṟiṉ peyarumāki, kāṟṟu eṉṟa colliṉ pakutiyumāyiṟṟu.
oḷiyiṉ etirmaṟiyākiya niḻalum tamiḻar marapil oruvaki oḷiyākavē koḷḷappaṭṭataṉāl iraṇṭum oṉṟukkoṉṟu col māṟṟākat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉ vaḻi niḻal oḷi eṉap poruḷpaṭṭatu pōla, kālam, kāḷam, niḻal, karumi eṉap poruḷpaṭṭu kariya uruviṉaḷākiya ulakattāy kāḷi eṉap paṭṭāḷ. kariyanañcu kakkum pāmpu kāḷiṅkaṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. vaṭamoḻi itaṉip peru vaḻakkākak koṇṭu taṉatākkik koḷḷiṉum ataṉ uṭaṉmoḻikaḷil iccol payilātataṉāl, atu intiyar mutal-tāymoḻiyām paḻantamiḻ allatu tirāviṭa moḻiyēyām eṉpatu teḷivākum.
paṭi paṭuttal-kollutal eṉṟa poruḷ tarum toḻiṟpeyar. itu ākupeyarāyp paṭukkum karuviyiyum, poruḷāku peyarāyp paṭukkum maṉitat tokutiyiyum kāṭṭiṟṟu. paṭaṉ eṉṟa vaṭacol itaṉ tiripēyākum eṉak koḷḷalām.
karu eṉpatu uri eṉpatu pōṉṟa uriccollaṭi, iyaṟkiyil piṟappiliruntē varum iyalpik karu  eṉṟum piṉvarum vaḷarvi uri eṉṟum tamiḻar  kūṟip poruḷkaḷik karupporuḷ eṉṟum uripporuḷ eṉṟum vakuttaṉar. itaṉuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiya piṟa taṉit tamiḻc coṟkaḷ karutu, karuttu eṉpaṉa. (karumam eṉṟa colkūṭa vaṭa moḻiyiṉattil illāta vaṭacol ātalāl ivaṟṟuṭaṉotta iyaṟkic col eṉak koḷḷap paṭalām). piṟappil iyalpāy varum paṇpukaḷ ēṟpaṭuvatu aṉṉi vayiṟṟil ātaliṉ, atu karu eṉṟum. piḷḷi vaḷarum pi karuppi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭatu. vaṭamoḻi ‘karppam’ eṉṟa col mūlamaṟṟa col; atu tamiḻc colliṉ tiripēyātal vēṇṭum.
kuvitaliṉ iṉṉoru vaṭivam kavital. ittuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiya coṟkaḷ kaviki, kaviḻ, kavar, kavaṭu mutaliyavi ākum; ivaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya iru coṟkaḷ kaviñaṉ, kaviti eṉpaṉa; kavarcciyuṭiyaṉa kaviti ākum.
kavaṭu eṉpatu iru piḷavāṉa kompu. kavar eṉṟum ulaka vaḻakku uṇṭu. ataṉ coṟpōlit tiripu kapaṭu. iraṇṭaka neñcatti, atāvatu  vañcattik kāṭṭum vaṭamoḻic col kapaṭam itaṉ tiripu.
mēṟkūṟiya vakiyil coṟkaḷ palavum perukkik koṇṭē pōkalām. collārāyccit tuṟiyil muṉipavarkaḷ ivaṟṟi oru potu ellik kōṭāka maṭṭum koṇṭu ārāyntāl vaṭamoḻiyiṉum pallūḻi toṉmi yum; iṉṟu kāṇappeṟum ciṟṟilakkiyam, ciṟṟaḷavu kaṭantu pēraḷavum uṭiya tamiḻ moḻip paṟṟip palappala uṇmikaḷ kāṇṭal ākum.
iṉṟum āḻntu ārāyntāl vaṭamoḻiyil iṭu kuṟiyākak kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷ tamiḻil aḻakiya tiṭpamuṭiya kāraṇac coṟkaḷō eṉṟu aiyuṟutaṟkiṭamiruppatu kāṇalām. tilliyampalattiṉ pēr citamparam eṉappaṭu kiṟatu. cit amparam, eṉṟu pirikkappaṭṭu cit-uyir, amparam-vāṉ; uyirākiya vāṉil ulavuvatu eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻar itaṉic ciṟṟampalam eṉa eḻututal marapu eṉpatu yāvarum aṟinta ceyti. itu tamiḻar paṇṭiya tavaṟukaḷuḷ oṉṟō eṉa innāḷil aiyuṟuvār uṇṭu. āṉāl, atu  tavaṟaṉṟu. ēṉeṉil, ciṟṟampalattiṉ etirccol pērampalam. eṉavē, ellā uyirkkum potuvāṉa potu veḷiyip pērampalam eṉṟum, ōr uyiriṉ uḷḷa veḷiyic ciṟṟampalam eṉṟum tamiḻar kūṟiyiruttal kaṇkūṭu. uyiri lum potu uyirākiya iṟivaṉip pēruyir eṉṟun taṉi uyirkaḷic ciṟ ṟuyirkaḷ eṉṟum aḻittaṉar. ciṟṟuyir eṉpatē vaṭavar moḻiyil ‘cit’eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatō eṉa eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu. ivvuyiriṉ nuṇ uruvam ‘veḷḷi poṉ mēṉiya tokkum’ eṉṟār yōkak kuṟaḷ kaṇṭa oḷaviyār. ataṟ kēṟpa aṭiyār itayattiliṟivaṉ naṭamiṭuvatu poṉṉampala naṭippu eṉṉap paṭum.
iṉṉum cittiram eṉpatu ōviyattiṉ maṟupeyar eṉṟum, vaṭacol eṉṟum koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl avaṉ cittiramākap pēcukiṟāṉ eṉṟa  ulaka vaḻakkil aḻakākap pēcukiṟāṉ eṉṟa potup poruḷiviṭa, nuṇuki nuṇukic ciṉṉañ ciṟu nayan tōṉṟap pēcukiṟāṉ eṉpatē teḷḷat teḷiyak kāṇap peṟukiṟatu. eṉavē cittiram eṉpatu “ciṟu tiṟam” ‘ciṟṟuttiram’ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ paṇṭiya marūuvō eṉṟu eṇṇalām. tiṟam iṅṅaṉam tiram āyiṟṟu eṉak koḷvōmāṉāl ‘cū°tiram’ ‘cūttiram’ eṉṟa vaṭacoṟkaḷ ‘cūḻccittiṟam’ ‘cūḷtiṟam’ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ marūuvō eṉṟum, vēttiram (pirampu) eṉpatu vēyttiṟamō eṉṟum aiyuṟalākum. 
mēṟkūṟiya iṭaṅkaḷilellām tamiḻar ittamiḻc col mūlaṅkaḷ vakukkāmal irukka vaṭamoḻiyāḷar maṭṭum vakuttatu ēṉ eṉṟu kēṭkalām. ilakkiyat toṭakkak kālam toṭṭu oru vakiyil tamiḻ ilakkiyattilum ilakkaṇattilum vaṭamoḻiyāḷariṉ naccukkoṭi paṭarntu ataṉ muṉṉiya ceḻumiyiyum taṉṉāṇmiyiyum paṭippaṭiyāy uṟiñci vantiruppatu kāṇalām. āṉāl, ārāycci nōkkuṭaṉ nōkkuvārkku ataṉiṭiyēyum tamiḻiṉ tolniliyi viḷakkum tolivaṟikuṟikaḷ tōṟṟāmal pōkā. tamiḻar  ‘ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē’ eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē koṇṭiruntaṉar. āṉāl, puttar camaṇar tōṉṟiyaviṭattaṉṟi intiyāvil eṅkum tāymoḻi vaḷam peṟṟatilli. tamiḻil puttar camaṇar tōṉṟumuṉ vaḷam peṟṟirunta ilakkiya ilakkaṇam puttar camaṇa āriyar kippaṭṭu avarkaḷ vaṭamuṟikaḷukkiciya vakukkappaṭṭatu. avvaṇṇam vakukkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷē pōṟṟappaṭṭu muṉṉit taṉit tamiḻp peru nūlkaḷ aḻintaṉa, aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. apperun tamiḻ nūlkaḷil ilakkaṇap perumikku oru tolkāppiyamum vāḻkkip perumikku oru tirukkuṟaḷum ilakkiyap perumikku  oru cilappatikāramum namakku mīntaṉa. acciṟu pala kaṇikaḷ mūlam  muṉṉiya perumiyiṟ palavaṟṟi nām uyttaṟiyalām. eṉavē tolkāppiyam moḻiyārāyccikku ōraḷavu payaṉpaṭum. ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ ilakkiyattil piṟkālattār otukkiya paḻañ coṟkaḷē yātalāl aviyum, tamiḻiṉ uṭaṉ moḻiyākiya teluṅku, maliyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliyaviyum, avaṟṟilum cilavakiyil tūyaviyāyirukkak kūṭum. vaṭanāṭṭu malivāḻ kuṭikaḷ, palūccikaḷ moḻikaḷum muṉṉiya tamiḻt tolvaṭivaṅkaḷi viḷakkavum col mūlaṅkaḷi muṭivu kaṭṭavum utavum eṉṉalām.


9. eḻuttuc cīrtirutta muṟikaḷ 
intiya makkaḷiṭiyē mikavum parantu kāṇappaṭum aṟiyāmiyip pōkkuvataṟkāṉa tiṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ nāṭṭiṉ paḻiya eḻuttu muṟikaḷukkup patilāka rōmaṉ eḻuttu muṟiyip parappuvatum oṉṟākum eṉa aracāṅkattār karuti ati naṭimuṟikkuk koṇṭuvara ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceyvatākak koñca nāḷkaḷukku muṉpu ōr aṟikki veḷiyiṭṭiruntaṉar. ataṉpaṭi “mataṟā° kāliṅ” eṉṟa mātamiru muṟip pattiriki yuṭaṉ rōmaṉ eḻuttil acciṭap paṭṭa tuṇṭup piracuram oṉṟum veḷiyiṭappaṭumeṉṟu aṟivikkap paṭṭiruntatu.
taṟkāla nilimikkut takkapaṭi tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷil cīrtiruttam ēṟpaṭuttutal vēṇṭumeṉṟu  pala muyaṟcikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ivi taṟpōtu karutappaṭuvatu pōl tamiḻ eḻuttu muṟiyi muṟṟi lum vilakkip putumuṟi vakukkum muyaṟcikaḷ alla. ākavē, ittakiya putu eḻuttu muṟi avaciyamā eṉpatiyum, ataṉāl ēṟpaṭum ātāya iḻappukaḷ evi eṉpatiyum ārāyntu pārkkavēṇṭiyatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu ākum.
tiṭṭam tamiḻ moḻiyi maṭṭumē yallāmal intiyat tāy moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyumē pātippatāyiṉum tamiḻ moḻiyip paṟṟi maṭṭilumē ciṟappāka iṅkē ataṉi ārāyvōm.
ātāyaṅkaḷ
immuṟiyi ātarippavarkaḷ karuttil koṇṭa ātāyaṅkaḷ mikat teḷivāṉavi. avaṟṟuḷ mika mukkiyamāṉatu atu aṉupavamākak kalvi kaṟpati mikavum eḷitākkum eṉpatē.
tamiḻ ariccuvaṭiyiṉ motta eḻuttukaḷ  naṉṉūl kaṇakkuppaṭi  369 ākiṟatu. paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷil kaṟpikkappaṭum ariccuvaṭiyiluḷḷapaṭi iv veṇṇikki 247 ākum. āṉāl, rōmaṉ muṟiyil eḻuttukaḷiṉ eṇṇikki 26 tāṉ.
iṉṉum vēṟu pala ātāyaṅkaḷum immuṟiyil iruppatākak karutappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. accuk kōppavarkaḷukkum kiyaccu (ṭip) aṭippavar kaḷukkum eḻuttukaḷiṉ eṇṇikki kuṟiviṉāl evvaḷavō naṉmiyum vacatiyum uṇṭu. kiyaccu aṭikkum iyantiramum mikac curuṅkiya aḷavil amikkappaṭu vatuṭaṉ accu niliyaṅkaḷilum accukaḷ toki kuṟiyum.
mēlum intiyāviluḷḷa eṇṇaṟṟa moḻip pētaṅkaḷi varivaṭivilēṉum itu mikavum kuṟikkum. oru moḻiyāḷar pēcuvatiyō karutu vatiyō maṟṟa moḻi pēcupavar aṟiyamuṭiyāviṭṭālum, ūrp peyarkaḷum, āḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum ellā moḻikaḷukkum potuvāy viṭṭa ciṉimā, mōṭṭār, kavarṉar mutaliya coṟkaḷum pēccil eppaṭip potuvāy irukkiṉ ṟaṉavō, appaṭiyē eḻuttilum eḷitil ellā moḻiyārālum potuvāka aṟiyappaṭum coṟkaḷāyviṭum. putitāka moḻi kaṟpavar oru cila coṟkaḷi yaṟintavuṭaṉ potuvarivaṭivaṅkaḷi vācittu uṇarak kūṭum.
rōmaṉ muṟiyil accukkotta accu eḻuttu, eḻutuvataṟkuriya kiyeḻuttu eṉṟum iruvaki uṇṭu. iṉṉum ittakiya pala ātāyaṅ kaḷum rōmaṉ eḻuttu muṟikku ātāramākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
naṭimuṟiyilēṟpaṭum cikkalkaḷ
mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟil pintik kūṟappaṭṭa cillaṟi ātāyaṅkaḷ uṇmiyilēyē ātāyaṅkaḷtām eṉpati maṟuppatiṟkilli. accukkum kiyaccu aṭippataṟkum iṉṟiya niliyil tāy moḻiyiṉ eḻuttukaḷ tokiyilum vaṭivilum cikkaluṭiyaviyē. tamiḻip paṟṟiya maṭṭil atu rōmaṉ eḻutti viṭa mikutiyāyiṉum piṟa intiya moḻikaḷi nōkkat  toki kuṟintatē eṉṟu kūṟalām. mēlum tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷukkuc ciṟitaḷavu cīrtiruttam ceytu iccillaṟik kuṟikaḷik kūṭap pōkkavum muṭiyum.
ittakiya cīrtiruttaṅkaḷiviṭa rōmaṉ eḻuttu muṟiyi uyarvākak karutuvōr atil atikappaṭiyāy iruppatāka eṇṇum ātāyaṅkaḷ iraṇṭu tām: avi eḻuttu eṇṇikkik kuṟivu. ellā moḻikaḷiṉ varivaṭivum oṉṟupaṭutal ākiyaviyē yākum.
eḻuttuk kuṟivu
mutalāvatāka eḻuttuk kuṟivi eṭuttuk koḷvōm. rōmaṉ eḻuttu muṟiyil 26 eḻuttukaḷ eṉṟu kūṟumpōtu nām āṅkila moḻikkut tēviyāṉa āṅkila ariccuvaṭiyiṉ eḻuttukaḷiyē eṇṇukiṟōm. ivaṟṟuḷ (o, ka, ṇa, b, pa, ṉa, ḷū, ṟa, ākiya) 8 eḻuttukaḷ tamiḻukkut tēvi yilli. āṉāl itaṟku māṟākat tamiḻ ariccuvaṭiyil uḷḷa eḻuttukaḷil rōmaṉ eḻuttu muṟiyil illāta eḻuttukaḷ pala. uyirkaḷiṉ neṭilkaḷ 7-m valliṉattil ca, ṭa, ṟa eṉṟa 3-m melliṉattil ṅa,ña, ṇa, ṉa ākiya    4-m iṭi eḻuttil ḻa, ḷa, ākiya 2-m āka 16 eḻuttukaḷ rōmaṉ muṟiyil illi.
mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi tamiḻil atikamāyuḷḷa eḻuttukaḷi rōmaṉ muṟiyil iraṇṭu vakiyāka eḻutalām. oṉṟu avaṟṟil cila kuṟiyīṭukaḷic cērttuk koḷvatu: utāraṇamāka (n) eḻuttiṉ mēl puḷḷiyiṭṭāl ‘ṅ’  eṉṟum ataṉkīḻ puḷḷiyiṭṭāl ‘ṇ’ eṉṟum koḷḷalām. āṉāl, immuṟi eḻuttu mayakkattiyum eḻuttup piḻikaḷiyum maliyac ceyvatuṭaṉ, olikaḷiyum mayaṅka vikkum.
eḻutuvatilum, vācippatilum itu mikavum ciramatti uṇṭu paṇṇuvatuṭaṉ rōmaṉ muṟiyiṉ ātāyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākak kūṟappaṭum eḻuttuc curukkattiyum itu aṟavē oḻittuviṭum. ēṉeṉil, ariccuvaṭiyil veḷip pārvikku (n) eṉṟa orē eḻuttēyiruntālum, accukkum, kiyaccukkum kuṟiyīṭukaḷuṭaṉkūṭiya attakiya pala eḻuttukaḷ uṇmiyilē tēvippaṭum.
tamiḻiluḷḷa atikappaṭi eḻuttik kuṟikka iṉṉoru muṟi kiyāḷappaṭalām. tamiḻil tēviyaṟṟa eḻuttukaḷi atikappaṭi eḻuttukaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷāka vaḻaṅkalām. ippaṭi vaḻaṅkiṉāl rōmaṉ eḻuttiṉ mukkiya māṉa ātāyam iraṇṭāvatu poyyāyviṭum. tamiḻukku vēṇṭāta eḻuttukaḷ maṟṟat tāymoḻikaḷukkut tēviyāṉaviyākum. ākavē, oli oṟṟumi keṭṭuppōkum. mēlum tēviyaṟṟa eḻuttukaḷ 8 tām: atikappaṭi eḻuttukaḷ tamiḻilēyē 16. maṟṟat tāymoḻikaḷil iṉṉum mikuti.
eḻuttup perukkattāl ēṟpaṭum aruvaruppukaḷ
itu tavira, rōmaṉ eḻuttukaḷil ariccuvaṭiyiltāṉ eḻuttukaḷil eṇṇikki kuṟivu. eḻutumpōtu coṟkaḷi amippataṟkāṉa eḻuttukaḷ virivākap pakkak kaṇakkil niṟittu aruvaruppiyē uṇṭākkum. irukkiṟatu eṉṟa  colli eḻuta aicaramamaciyavara eṉak kuṟintatu 10 eḻuttukaḷ tēvippaṭum. itu eḻutuvataṟkum tolli, paṭippataṟkum eḷitaṉṟu. kuṟiyīṭukaḷuṭaṉ cērntu eḻutuvatāṉālum tolli iṉṉum paṉmaṭaṅkākum.
mēlum, uyirum meyyum cērntu tamiḻil orē eḻuttāka eḻutap paṭukiṟatu. rōmaṉ muṟiyil ivaṟṟip piritteḻututal vēṇṭum. tamiḻariṭiyēyum piṟa intiyat tāymoḻi yāḷariṭiyēyum, paṭittavar kaḷukkukkūṭa uyir mey eḻutti uyirum meyyumākap pirittuṇarva teṉpatu kallil nārurippatu  pōl kaṭiṉamāṉa kāriyam. airōppāvil ciṟupiḷḷip paruvattiliruntē peritum uruvēṟṟappaṭṭu viṭum inta muṟiyi intiyaraṉivarum-atilum ciṟappākak kirāmaṅkaḷil vāḻum pāmara makkaḷ eḷitil piṉpaṟṟa māṭṭārkaḷ eṉpati avarkaḷiṭiyē āciriyarkaḷā yiruntu pōtaṉi naṭattiyavarkaḷtām eḷitil aṟivārkaḷ.
mēṟkūṟiya ceytikaḷāl rōmaṉ muṟiyiliruppatākac collappaṭum ātāyaṅkaḷ ārāyntu pārkkap pōṉāl ātāyaṅkaḷēyalla. atu maṭṭu maṉṟu. atil iḻappukkaḷkūṭa mikutiyāyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kāṇalām. ciṟappākak kaṭṭāyak kalvikku utaviyāka atu caṟṟum  payaṉpaṭāteṉṟē tōṟṟukiṟatu. avvakiyil rōmaṉ muṟiyi viṭa nām mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa cillaṟic cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ mikutiyāṉa payaṉ tarum eṉṟum kūṟalām.
cillaṟic cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ
tamiḻ eḻuttukkup puṟampāṉa rōmaṉ muṟi allatu nākarika muṟi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ utaviyillāmalē tamiḻil pala cīrtiruttaṅkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cila eḻuttukaḷ putitāyp paṭippavarkku ati eḷitākkuvatuṭaṉ, accukkum vēṇṭiyapaṭi eḻuttukaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyik kuṟikkum taṉmiyuṭiyavi. malāy nāṭṭilum  intiyāvilum veḷiyiṭap paṭum cila muṟpōkkāṉa tamiḻp pattirikikaḷ ākiya eḻuttukaḷi li, ṉi, ṇi, ḷi eḻuti varukiṉṟaṉa. itu puraṭcikaramāṉa māṟutal eṉṟu colvataṟkilli. putitāyp paṭippavarkaḷ niliyiliruntum, veḷināṭṭār niliyiliruntum pārttāl ittiruttam iyalpākat  tōṟṟu kiṟatu vaḻakkamāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷē putumiyākavum cikkal vāyntaviyākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉum ākiya eḻuttukaḷum itē muṟiyil ap pattirikikaḷil ṉā, ṟā, ṇā eṉṟu eḻutap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. accakattukkum kiyaccukkum itaṉāl ēṟpaṭum ātāyam koñcamaṉṟu. ēṉeṉil ivaṟṟukkeṉa amikkappaṭṭa 8 accukaḷ mīntuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. itē cīrtiruttatti iṉṉum caṟṟu virivupaṭuttuvatāṉāl, meyyuṭaṉ u, ū cērumiṭaṅkaḷil varum kuṟiyīṭṭiyum orē vaḻippaṭuttalām. kūṭumāṉāl vaṭaveḻuttukaḷāṉa ṣu, hu eṉpavaṟṟilum sū, ṣū, hū eṉpavaṟṟilum kiyāḷappaṭum vaṭivattiyē mēṟkoṇṭu viṭṭāl accukaḷil irupatuk katikamākak kuṟivu ēṟpaṭum. cuḻiyiṭāta ‘u’, cuḻiyiṭṭa ‘u’ ākiyavaṟṟit talikīḻākki ‘u, ū’ uyir mey kuṟiyīṭukaḷākkiviṭṭāl,  ātāyam iṉṉum mikum. uruvamum ceyam uṭiyatākum. paṭippavarkaḷukkum eḻutupavarkaḷukkum ekarattiṟkāṉa kompuk kuṟiyīṭṭi eḻuttukaḷukku muṉṉāl cērppataṟkup patil piṉṉāl cērttuk ke eṉpataṟkum patil kab eṉṟeḻutuvatu kūṭa iyaṟki neṟiyi oṭṭiya cīrtiruttamēyākak kūṭum eṉṟu eṇṇu kiṟōm.
piṟamoḻi olikaḷ
iṟutiyāka rōmaṉ muṟiyillāta olikaḷukkuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷ cērttup putu olikaḷik kuṟikkak kūṭumāyiṉ, tamiḻ ariccuvaṭiyilillāta putu olikaḷukku aṅṅaṉam ceyyamuṭiyum eṉpatu teḷivē. perumpālum tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷ ovvoṉṟum tamakkeṉat teḷivāṉa oli vēṟupāṭukaḷi uṭiya viyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvakiyil rōmaṉ muṟi tamiḻ eḻuttu muṟiyiyum, piṟa intiyat tāymoḻikaḷiṉ eḻuttu muṟikaḷiviṭa evvaḷavō piṟpaṭṭatēyākum. ēṉeṉil, āṅkilattip paṟṟiya maṭṭil uyirkaḷil neṭil kuṟil vēṟṟumiyilli. atu pōka ovvōr uyirum avvappōtu oliyillātu vīṇē vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatuṭaṉ, vevvēṟāṉa 10 allatu 12 olikaḷ uṭiyaviyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. itupōka meykaḷilum pala ‘olikaḷ’ ōr eḻuttālum, pala eḻuttukaḷāl ōr oliyum kuṟikkappaṭṭu elliyaṟṟa kuḻappam viḷikiṟatu. tamiḻ mutaliya nāṭṭu eḻuttukaḷic cīrtirutti vaḻaṅkiṉāl ittakiya kuḻappaṅkaḷukku iṭamilli. immuṟiyil tamiḻar aṟiyumpaṭi tamiḻil illātu, āṅkilam mutaliya mēlnāṭṭu  moḻikaḷilum, vaṭamoḻi intu°tāṉi ākiya vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷilum uḷḷa olikaḷiyum tēvip paṭṭa  iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟittukkoḷḷalām.


10. tamiḻum tamiḻiṉa moḻikaḷum
pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷi ‘kaṉṉaṭamum, kaḷi teluṅkum, kaviṉ maliyāḷamum, tuḷuvum, uṉṉutarat tutitteḻuntē oṉṟu  pala  āyiṉa’ eṉṟu tamiḻmoḻiyi nōkkik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
āyiṉum, araciyal talivar cilarum, tamiḻp pulavar cilarum itik kūṟat tayaṅki varukiṟārkaḷ. itaṟkuk kāraṇam tamiḻaṉukku neṭunāḷāka ēṟpaṭṭirukkum tōlvi maṉappāṉmiyiyum, ‘añci añcic cākum’ accattiyum palar payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭatē yākum. “tamiḻar ippaṭit tam pukaḻ pāṭi eṉṉa payaṉ?” ‘cariyāṉa pulamiyeṉil piṟa nāṭṭār ati vaṇakkam ceytal vēṇṭum’ `icceytiyi ēṟṟukkoḷḷum maliyāḷi, kaṉṉaṭiyaṉ, teluṅkaṉ yār’ eṉṟu avarkaḷ kēṭkiṟārkaḷ.
iti oppukkoḷḷum maliyāḷi, kaṉṉaṭiyar, teluṅkar uṇṭu. atuvum potuppaṭiyāṉa āṭkaḷkūṭa allar. kallūriyum, palkalikkaḻaka mum oppum  mutaltarat talivarkaḷ. attakiyōr urikaḷil cilavaṟṟik kāṇpōm.
mutalil oru maliyāḷa aṟiñar uriyik kīḻē tarukiṟōm.
tiruvitāṅkūr aracariṉ maliyāḷa moḻiyāciriyarāṉa ē. kiruṣṇa piṣāraṭi 1927 mārccil ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka ātaravil ‘maliyāḷa moḻiyum, ilakkiyamum’ eṉṟa ārāyccic coṟpoḻiviṭiyē maliyāḷa moḻiyil kūṟiyatit tamiḻ eḻuttil appaṭiyē tarukiṟōm.
maliyāḷac coṟpoḻivu
“teluṅku, tamiḻ, karṇṇāṭakam, maliyāḷam ennīvaka tramiṭa pāṣakaḷ oru tramiṭa mūla pāṣakku tannē kālakramattiluṇṭāya  pariṇāma pētaṅkaḷāyi varuvāṉē taramuḷḷu; ennu mātramalla, aṅṅaṉē oru mūlapāṣa uṇṭā yiruntu vennum, ā mūlapāṣakkuṇṭāyirunna pērāṇu tamiḻ ennuḷḷa tentum ....teḷiyippāṉ cila lakṣyaṅkaḷ tannēyu uṇṭu. onnāma tāyi, malayāḷa pāṣakku ‘malanāṭṭut tamiḻ’ ennum, karṇṇāṭaka pāṣakkuk ‘karināṭṭut tamiḻ’ ennum, cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷilē pāṣakku ‘cōḻat tamiḻ’ ennum, ‘pāṇṭit tamiḻ’ ennum paṟañcu (kūṟappaṭṭu) vannirunnatāyi  paḻiya tamiḻ krantaṅkaḷilum malayāḷa krantaṅkaḷilum kāṇunnatu koṇṭu tannē tamiḻ ennatutramiṭa pāṣakaḷuk kellām potuvāya pēr āṇu ennum, vicēṣattēk kāṇippāṉāṇu malanāṭṭut tamiḻ, karināṭṭut tamiḻ, enta maṭṭil orē vicēṣaṇam (aṭimoḻi) cērttup paṟayunnatu ennum uḷḷatu °paṣmāṇu.... ‘tamiḻ nāṭṭu ai vēntarum vantār’ enniṅṅaṉē, cōḻam, pāṇṭiyam, kēraḷam, karṇāṭakam, teluṅku, ī añcu rājākkaḷmārē tamiḻiṉṟē prati °tāpakaṉmārāyi (tamiḻi nili niṟuttiyavarāy) kaṇiccum koṇṭu centamiḻ krantaṅkaḷil pratipātic ciṭṭuḷḷatum (ārāyntu niṟuviyuḷḷatum) malayāḷa pāṣayilē prācīṉa (paṇṭiya) krantaṅṅaḷāya ‘amaram tamiḻ,’ ‘nampiyāruṭē tamiḻ’ mutalāya pērukaḷ kāṇunnatum, itiṉṉutā haraṅṅaḷāṉu......līlā tilakam enna prācīṉa kranta kāraṉā kaṭṭē, maṇip piravāḷa lakṣaṇa pra°tāvattil malayāḷa pāṣayum, cama°krutavum kūṭic cērt tuṇṭākkiya kāvyattiṉāṇu maṇi pravāḷamennu paṟaññu varunna tennuḷḷatiṉṉu  pramāṇamāyi (mēṟkōḷāy) ‘tamiḻ maṇi, cam°krutam pavaḻam’ ennum maṟṟum pala prayōkaṅkaḷum kāṇiccu avayilē tamiḻ captattiṉu (collukku) malayāḷa pāṣa ennatu tramiṭa pāṣakaḷuk kellām potuvāya pērāṇu ennum °paṣṭamāyi paṟaññiṭṭu muṇṭu.”
mēṟkāṭṭiya mēṟkōḷ maliyāḷamāyiruntum tamiḻarukkup purivatutāṉ eṉṟu kāṇalām.  uṇmiyil tamiḻilakkaṇam terintavar eḻutum tamiḻi viṭa, tamiḻaṉ pēcum tamiḻukku im maliyāḷam mikavum aṭuttiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
teluṅku moḻiyilum vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ pukuttappaṭṭa varalāṟu; vaṭacoṟkaḷik kalantapiṉ accoṟkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟit tūya teluṅkuc coṟkaḷiyum vaṭacol eṉac cātikkac ceyyappaṭum muyaṟci ākiyavaṟṟip paṟṟi oru taliciṟanta teluṅku moḻiyaṟiñar kūṟṟukaḷik kīḻē tarukiṟōm.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattil teluṅkiṉ mutaṉmi āyvuri yāḷarāṉa koraṭā rāmakiruṣṇayyā em. ē. avarkaḷ tirāviṭa moḻi nūlārāyccikaḷ (1935) eṉṟa nūlilum tirāviṭa moḻip potuc coṟkaḷ (1944) eṉṟa nūlilum, kīḻvarumāṟu āṅkilattil kuṟippiṭuvatit tamiḻil moḻipeyarttut tarukiṟōm.
tirāviṭa moḻi nūlārāyccikaḷ (pak.42) teluṅkil mutal kaviñaṉ naṉṉayarē yāyiṉum avar vaṭamoḻip paṟṟāḷarāṉamiyāl (vaṭamoḻi kalanta) kaṭunaṭiyil 100-kku 75 vaṭacoṟkaḷuṭaṉ pāratatti moḻi peyarttār. āyiṉum avar piṉ vanta kaviñarkaḷ palar, ciṟappākac civap pulavarkaḷ ittakiya peruvāriyāṉa vaṭamoḻi iṟakkumatiyik kaṇṭittu ‘jāṉu’ teluṅku (tūya teluṅku) naṭiyi ātarittaṉar. ittakiya teluṅkiyē nāṭṭup potu makkaḷ eḷitāka uṇarntukoḷḷa muṭiyum  eṉpatu palkuṟiki cōmanātar kōṭpāṭu... mēlum tikkaṇar ‘vaḻaṅkāta putuccoṟkaḷi’ vaḻaṅkuvatu tavaṟu eṉṟu kūṟiyavarē yāyiṉum, uḷḷārnta tēciya maṉap pāṉmi uṭiyavarā yiruntamiyāl naṉṉayariviṭa mikutiyāṉa tūyateluṅku allatu tēciya moḻic coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉār. āyiṉum, vaṭa col vaḻakku mikuti kāraṇamāka ittēciyac coṟkaḷ mēlum vaḻaṅkātu pōṉataṉāl iṉṟu tikkaṇariṉ tēciya naṭi kaṭumiyākavum, naṉṉayariṉ kaṭu vaṭamoḻi naṭi eḷitākavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻiṉa moḻip potuc coṟkaḷ
intiyāvil pēcappaṭum ellā moḻikaḷumē vaṭamoḻi allatu āriya mūlaṅkaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟa nampikki potuvāka intiya (āriya) aṟiñariṭiyēyum ilakkaṇap pulavariṭiyēyum iruntu varukiṟatu. uṇmiyil teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷil peruvāriyāka vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu vanta coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉpatu uṇmiyēyāyiṉum, tēciyap pakuti (tūya tāymoḻip pakuti)yē atil muṉinta  pakutiyāyuḷḷatu; itaṉi ilakkaṇa nūlārum ottuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.  inniliyilum, teluṅku moḻiyilum, piṟa teṉintiya moḻikaḷilum uḷḷa coṟkaḷellā vaṟṟiyum vaṭamoḻi allatu pirākirutattiliruntu varuvittuk kāṭṭavum, ataṉ  mūlam vaṭamoḻi, pirākirutam ākiyavaṟ ṟiliruntē immoḻikaḷ tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟa, taṅkaḷ maṉattilūṟikkiṭakkum eṇṇatti valuppaṭuttavum avarkaḷ muyalkiṉṟaṉar. vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa col mūlaṅkaḷ (cuṭiṭivaḷa) palvēṟu muṉ iṭiccoṟkaḷuṭaṉ palvēṟupaṭṭa poruḷ koḷḷat takkaviyāyiruppatāl enta moḻic colliyum atu evvuruvamuṭiyatāyiṉum, poruḷuṭiya tāyiṉum vaṭamoḻikkiṇiyat tirittuk kuḻappamuṭikiṟatu. vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu piṟamoḻikaḷi varuvikka eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭum, immuyaṟciyiṉ piḻi pāṭṭi ki.pi.7ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta vaṭamoḻi yaṟiñarāṉa kumārilapaṭṭar kaṇṭittu eccarittuḷḷār. 
kumārilapaṭṭar kūṟuvatāvatu: tūya tirāviṭac coṟkaḷākiya cōṟu, pāmpu, vayiṟu, atar (pāti) mutaliyavaṟṟi vaṭamoḻip pakutikaḷiṉ oliyuṭaṉum avaṟṟiṉ poruḷuṭaṉum oṭṭavittu varuvikka eṇṇuvatu porutta maṟṟatu. (avar vaṭamoḻik kūṟṟi appaṭiyē tamiḻil tarukiṟōm).
tatyatō otaṉam cōṟu ityuktē cōra patvācyam kalpayanti; pantānam atar ityukkē ‘atara’ iti kalpayitvā āṣūt  catyam tu°travāt atara ēvapantāiti. tatā pāmpu captam pakārāntam carpa, vacaṉam anārāntam kalpayit vācatyam pāpa ēva aṅkaḷa iti vatanti; ēvam vayiṟu captam rēpāntam utaravacaṉam viri captē naprat yām nāyavatanti.
mēlum teluṅkil mōvi (-muṉvāy allatu utaṭu), mōkālu  (-muḻaṅkāl allatu muṭṭu), pokkili (pokkuḷ allatu koppuḷ-poṟ kuḻi), kōvalu (kōyil-kō-il; iṟivaṉ vīṭu),  ciluvā (cilu-vāy-vāyil nañcuṭiyatu; pāmpu), iṉumu (irumpu-poṉ-kariya ulōkam) eṉṟa coṟkaḷ pirikka muṭiyātu māṟiviṭṭapaṭiyiṉāl  paḻantamiḻp poruḷiḻantu viṭṭaṉa eṉṟum aṟiñar irāmakiruṣṇiyā kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
vaṭacolli mutalil pukutti ataṉpiṉ ellām vaṭacol eṉṟu kūṟa muṉiyum vaṭāriyar cūḻcciyi itteluṅkaṟiñarum vaṭamoḻi yaṟiñarum kaṇṭippatu pōlavē maliyāḷa moḻiyi ārāynta mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñar ṭākṭar kuṇṭarṭṭum kaṇṭittuḷḷār. avar kūṟuvatāvatu: “tirāviṭa moḻic coṟkaḷ muḻuvatiyum-ēṉ atil kalantuḷḷa arapumoḻi, airōppiya moḻic coṟkaḷiyum kūṭa-ellām vaṭa moḻiyiliruntu tirintu vantaviyē eṉṟu kūṟum intiyap ‘pulavarkaḷ’ ettaṉiyō pērik kāṇalām. āyiṉum vaṭamoḻiyil tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ kalantuḷḷaṉa eṉpati ottukkoḷḷum intiyap pulavarkaḷi eṅkum kāṇamuṭiyavilli.”
kuṇṭarṭ annāḷ intiyariṭam kaṇṭa kuṟikaḷ aṟiñariṭiyē kuṟintu varukiṟatu. āṉāl, potu makkaḷiṭiyē aṟiñar aṟivu iṉṉum parava viṭātu āriyap paḷḷikaḷ, āriyap pattirikikaḷ, āriya niliyaṅkaḷ taṭuttu varukiṉṟaṉa.


11. tirukkuṟaḷum vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷum
tiruvaḷḷuvar tamiḻulakukku aḷitta muppālamiḻtam ellāc camayattukkum, ellā nāṭṭu makkaṭkum maṭṭumaṉṟi mukkālattukkum potuvāṉa muḻu mutal tamiḻ nūl. ittakiya cīriya ciṟappuṭiya nūl ulakil vēṟemmoḻiyilum illi eṉpatu kaṇṭu, tamiḻar kaḷippaṭikiṉṟaṉar.
‘veṟemmoḻiyilum’ eṉpatil vaṭamoḻiyum aṭaṅkittāṉ kiṭakkiṟatu. ati maṟuppārum illi. vēṟemmoḻiyilum eppulavarkkum tarappaṭāta  ‘teyvap pulavar’ eṉṟa puṉi peyarum tiruvaḷḷuvarkkē ciṟappāṉa peyarāy vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. teyva moḻi eṉṟu taṉṉālum, piṟa palarālum koḷḷappaṭṭa vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu ataṟku-allatu-atil kaṇṭa karuttukku mūlam tēṭap perumpāṭupaṭṭa uriyāciriyar parimēlaḻakarē tammiyum mīṟit tam nāvāra, uḷamāra avarit teyvap pulavar eṉṟu kūṟik kikūppi vaṇaṅkiyirukkiṉṟār.
tiruvaḷḷuvar tirukkuṟaḷiṉ māṇpip piṟkālattavar eḷitil uṇarumpaṭi ‘maṇi uri’ eḻutiya parimēlaḻakap periyārkkut tamiḻakam evvaḷavō kaṭamippaṭṭiruppatu uṇmi. āṉāl, avar tēṭiya vaṭamoḻi mūlaṅkaḷ uṇmiyil tiruvaḷḷuvarkku mūlaṅkaḷ āyirukkak kūṭumā?-allatu-atu muṟṟilum avar vaṭamoḻip paṟṟiṉ viḷivā-? eṉpatit tamiḻaṉpar ārāytal amivuṭiyatē.
tamiḻar itaṉi ārāyvatil mikuti akkaṟi kāṭṭātatu iyalpē. pālamiḻtam kaṇṭaviṭattu  ati uṇpati viṭṭu, atu yār camittatu eṉṟu aṟiyak kāttiruppavaṉ aṟivāḷiyākamāṭṭāṉ. tamiḻarkku aṟivoḻuṅku kāṭṭiya tiruvaḷḷuvarum.
`‘epporuḷ yāryārvāyk kēṭpiṉum, apporuḷ
  meypporuḷ kāṇpa taṟivu’’
eṉṟu tamiḻaṉukku aṟivuri kūṟiyuḷḷār. āyiṉum iv vaṭamoḻi mūlac ceyti aṭikkaṭi tamiḻaṉ virumpiṉālum, virumpāviṭṭālum tali nīṭṭi varukiṟatu. aṇmiyil tamiḻttuṟiyil perum poṟuppilirunta  periyār oruvar iv vaṭamoḻi mūlac ceytiyip peritupaṭutti valiyuṟutti yatuṭaṉ nillātu tiruvaḷḷuvar, iḷaṅkō, cīttaliccāttaṉār mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ kālavariyaṟiyiyum akkaruttukkēṟpa māṟṟa viḻivatāl icceytiyit tamiḻaṉ mīṭṭum ārāyntu  uṇmi etu eṉak kāṇutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
āṉāl, ivvārāycciyil pukumuṉ tamiḻarkaḷ oru ceytiyi maṟavā tiruttal vēṇṭum. ivvārāycci muṭipu etuvāyiṉum atu tiruvaḷḷuvar perumiyi oru ciṟitum acittuviṭātu. vaṭamoḻiyilāyiṉum cari, vēṟem moḻiyilāyiṉum cari, itaṉip pōṉṟa-itaṉi yotta nūl tiruk kuṟaḷukku muṉṉō, piṉṉō camakālattilō (atāvatu mukkālattilum) kiṭiyātu eṉṟa nili ellārumoppa muṭinta muṭipēyākum.
mutalil parimēlaḻakar uriyil kaṇṭa ceytikaḷi eṭuttuk koḷvōm.
parimēlaḻakar tirukkuṟaḷi oppuyarvaṟṟa muḻu mutal nūl-allatu-mutaṉūl eṉṟu koṇṭārēyaṉṟi vaḻi nūl eṉṟu koṇṭār eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu. kuṟaḷāciriyari avar teyvappulavar eṉṟār. atu vaḻi nūlāṉāl ataṉ mutaṉūlāciriyarkaḷit tiruvaḷḷuvari viṭac ciṟappittuk kūṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam  avar kūṟavilli eṉpatu teḷivu.
parimēlaḻakar kuṟippatellām tirukkuṟaḷiṉ karuttukaḷiṟ pala maṉunūl. cāṇakkiyar poruḷnūl, kāmāntaka nīti nūl ākiyavaṟṟiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē. kāla ārāycci ēṟpaṭāta akkālattil  vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷ ellām mikap paḻiyaṉa eṉṟu nampappaṭṭu vantaṉa. atu akkālac camayak koḷki eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām. eṉavē, arumpāṭupaṭṭu iv vaṭamoḻi orumippāṭukaḷik kaṇṭa parimēlaḻakar, tiruvaḷḷuvar innūlkaḷik kaṟṟuṇarntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟeṇṇiṉar. piṟkālat tamiḻar palar eṇṇuvatu pōl, avarkku itu tiruvaḷḷuvariṭam matippuk kuṟinta kāraṇattāl alla; parimēlaḻakar kālattil pulavar eṉṟa col vaṭamoḻip pulavarkkē mutalurimi yāyiruntatu. eṉavē, tiruvaḷḷuvariṭam parimēlaḻakar koṇṭa paṟṟutaliṉ kāraṇamākavē avar periya vaṭamoḻip pulavarākavum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu avar eṇṇiṉār.
parimēlaḻakar kāṭṭiya ivvoppumikaḷic cākkākak koṇṭu ikkāla ārāycciyāḷar cilar tirukkuṟaḷukku avvaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷ mūlaṅkaḷ-allatu-mutaṉūlkaḷ eṉṟu avar eṇṇiyatāka maṉappāl kuṭikkiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻar karuttuppaṭi mutaṉūl eḻutattakkavar kaṭavuḷ-allatu-kaṭavuḷ niliyiluḷḷa muṉivarkaḷ. vaḻinūl eḻutupavar avarvaḻi niṟkum aṟiñar ākiya maṉitarkaḷē. vaḻi nūlāciriyar mutaṉūṟ karuttukaḷic curukkiyum, virittum, eḷimippaṭuttiyum putumuṟippaṭuttalām. mutaṉūlukku  māṟupaṭavō, mikip paṭavō avar eḻutār. mutaṉūlāciriyariṉum avar  ciṟanta varākak koḷḷavum paṭār. inniliyil vittup pārttāl parimēlaḻakar eṇṇam teḷivākum. tiruvaḷḷuvar pulamikkup pārāṭṭākavē ivvoppumikaḷ tarappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl, vaḻinūl alla eṉṟa eṇṇatti vaṟpuṟuttavē avar ‘teyvap pulavar’ eṉṟa peyari valiyuṟutti  eṭuttāṇṭār. urippāyirattil avar moḻiyi uṟṟu nōkkuvārkku itu pulaṉām.
“ivaṟṟuḷ (atāvatu aṟattiṉ pirivukaḷākiya oḻukkam, vaḻakku, taṇṭam ākiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ) vaḻakkum, taṇṭamum ulaka neṟi niṟuttutaṟ payatta(ṉa) vāvatallatu, oḻukkam pōla makkaḷuyirkku uṟutipayattaṟ ciṟappila vākalāṉum, avitām nūlāṉēyaṉṟi uṇarvu mikutiyāṉum, tēyaviyaṟ kiyāṉum aṟiyappaṭutalāṉum; avaṟṟi oḻittu, īṇṭut teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvarāl ciṟappuṭiya oḻukkamē aṟameṉa eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu.”
mutaṉūl, vaḻinūl iyalpukaḷi uṉṉik kavaṉippavarkku mutaṉūlē  uṟutipayappatum, potu nūlumām eṉpatum, uṇarvu mikutiyāṉum, tēyaviyaṟkiyāṉum aṟiyappaṭattakka ceytikaḷi oṭṭik kūṟupaviyē vaḻi nūl, cārpu nūlkaḷām eṉpatum viḷaṅkum. ākavē, kālavariyaṟiyi nīkkip pārttāl parimēlaḻakar kūṟum iyalpukaḷiṉpaṭiyum, tiruvaḷḷuvar nūlē mutaṉūl eṉpataṟku aṭṭiyilli. tiruvaḷḷuvarnūlik kāla iyaṟkikkum, tēyaviyaṟkikkum takkapaṭi viritta cōmēcar mutumoḻi veṇpāp pōṉṟa nūlkaḷ tiruvaḷḷuvar nūluṭaṉ ettakiya uṟavuṭiyaviyō attakiya uṟavutāṉ iv vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷukkum. avi kālattāl tirukkuṟaḷukku muntiyaviyāmē eṉṉiṉ ataṉik kīḻē ārāyvōm.
itaṟkiṭiyil tamiḻarkaḷiṭiyē iṉṉoru viṉā eḻalām: ‘parimēlaḻakar tirukkuṟaḷiyē mutaṉūlākak koṇṭārāyiṉ vaṭamoḻik karuttu mūlaṅkaḷi ittaṉi arumpāṭupaṭṭut tēṭuvāṉēṉ-?’ eṉpatē akkēḷvi.
parimēlaḻakar vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ciṟanta pulavar. ivvoppumikaḷ tarappaṭṭatē ataṉik kāṭṭum. pulavarkaḷ tam pulami virivip payaṉpaṭutti oppumi kāṭṭal iṉṟum putitalla. caṅka nūlkaḷiṟ palavaṟṟi uriyuṭaṉum, uriyiṉṟiyum patippitta aṟiñar-u.vē.cāminātayyar avarkaḷ piṟa caṅka nūlkaḷ,cintāmaṇi, kamparāmāyaṇam, pāratam ākiyavaṟṟiliruntu oppumik kuṟippukaḷ tantirukkiṟār eṉpatu yāvarum aṟintatē.
āṉāl, aṟiñar-u.vē.cāminātayyar avarkaḷ pōla oppumi tanta tuṭaṉ parimēlaḻakar niṟuttik koḷḷavilli eṉpatu uṇmiyē. vaṭamoḻik karuttukaḷ tirukkuṟaḷukku muntiyavi eṉṟa tam iyaṟki eṇṇattiyum terivittirukkiṟār. 
‘iyaṟki eṇṇam’ eṉṟu kuṟittataṉ kāraṇam ikkāla ārāycciyāḷarpōl, avveṇṇattip  putitāka nilināṭṭa avar muyaṉṟavarallar eṉpataṟkē.
ivviyaṟki eṇṇam-allatu-nampikkiyāl tūṇṭap paṭṭa avar, oppumi kāṭṭiyatu avar pulamiyiṉ kāraṇamāka maṭṭumaṉṟu eṉpatu avar kāla nilimikaḷi aṟipavarkku maṭṭuntāṉ viḷaṅkum.
parimēlaḻakar kālam civa, viṇava camaya maṟumalarcciyum, vaṭamoḻi mēlāṇmiyum oruṅkē mēlōṅkiya kālam. civa, viṇava camayaṅkaḷiṉ paḻiya varalāṟṟik kavaṉittāl avi vētaneṟikkup puṟampāka, pala iṭaṅkaḷil etirākakkūṭa vaḷarntavi eṉṟu kāṇalām. āṉāl, putta, camaṇa camayaṅkaḷ avaṟṟiyum tāṇṭi vētaneṟiyi etirttapōtu, vēta neṟiyāḷar civa, viṇavari aṇittuk koṇṭu putta, camaṇa camayatti aḻippatil avarkaḷ ottuḻippip peṟṟaṉar. peṟṟālum, vēta neṟikku mutaliṭam peṟṟuviṭutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa kāriyattilum kaṇṇāyiruntu ataṟku avvappōtu civa, viṇavap pōṭṭiyip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar.
tamiḻariṭiyē puttam, camaṇam mēlōṅkiyapōtu vēta neṟikkuttāṉ iḻukku nērntatēyaṉṟit tirukkuṟaḷukku iḻukku nēravilli. putta nūlākiya maṇimēkaliyiṉ āciriyar avar kuṟaḷoṉṟi mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭi, avarip ‘poyyil pulavaṉ’ eṉṟu kuṟittirukkiṟār. puttariyē kuṟikkumpōtukūṭa āciriyar itaṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pukaḻuri taramuṭiyātu. camaṇa nūlkaḷ iṉṉum orupaṭi pōy avarit taṅkaḷ āciriyaruḷ oruvarākak koḷkiṉṟaṉa.
civa, viṇava maṟumalarcciyilum tiruvaḷḷuvar pukaḻukkuk kuṟivilli. civar avarip  poyyaṭimi yillāta pulavarkaḷuḷ iṭan tantatuṭaṉ nillātu 64 nāyaṉ māruḷḷum avari oruvarākkiṉar. viṇava āḻvārkaḷum avarip pārāṭṭap piṉvāṅkavilli. tamiḻnāṭṭil tamiḻariṭiyē eḻunta entak kaliyilum muḻup polivuṭaṉ ōṅkiyuyarntu mitanta pukaḻ tirukkuṟaḷiṉuṭiyatu.
